RhythmDesk（鼓谱工作台）开发交接文档
覆盖范围：1.59（接手时的版本）→ 1.60 → 1.61 → 1.62 → 1.63 → 1.64
编写日期：2026-11-22

==============================================================================
一、项目结构与两个平台的关系
==============================================================================
1. Windows 11 版（RhythmDesk-Win11/）
   · RhythmDesk.exe：启动器，用 Edge 打开同目录的 RhythmDesk.html（exe 本身未改动，不含版本号）。
   · RhythmDesk.html：单文件离线编辑器，由 source/build_ui.py 把 source/ui 下的
     index.html + style.css + i18n.js + model.js + presets.js + user-recordings.js + audio.js + score-export.js + app.js
     和 help/ 说明书（图片转 base64）拼装生成。修改代码只改 source/ui，再运行 python3 source/build_ui.py。
   · sounds/：音色（TimGM6mb，GPL-2.0），source/tests/：测试，source/iphone-offline/：iPhone 离线版打包脚本（本次只同步了版本号，未生成 iPhone 包，
     因为它需要的 RhythmDesk-iPhone-Web 网页包不在接手的压缩包中）。

2. 安卓版（RhythmDesk-Android-x.xx.apk，包名 org.rhythmdesk.app，minSdk 26，targetSdk 34）
   · 外壳 classes.dex（WebView）未改动；编辑器是 APK 内的 assets/index.html，它和 Win11 页面使用同一套脚本、样式和说明书，
     另含安卓专用代码（rhythmDeskAndroid 标志、系统保存窗口桥接、androidFollow* 跟随实现、内嵌音色数据、安卓版说明书文字）。
   · 接手的压缩包没有安卓 index.html 的拆分源码，因此每轮修改都写成补丁清单（tools/patch160.py … tools/patch164.py），
     同一份清单同时作用于 Win11 源码和安卓 index.html：每处修改必须在两边都恰好匹配规定次数，否则整个补丁不写入。
     这保证两个平台的改动完全一致。生成后还比对了「Win11 页面 与 安卓页面」的差异，确认只剩原有的安卓专用部分。
   · APK 重新打包：tools/repack_apk.py 以上一版已签名 APK 为基础，只替换 assets/index.html 和二进制 AndroidManifest.xml 中的
     versionCode/versionName，其余条目内容不变，resources.arsc 保持不压缩并 4 字节对齐。
   · 签名：tools/SignApk.java 调用 com.android.tools.build:apksig:2.3.0（Maven Central）以 APK Signature Scheme v2 签名
     （与接手时 1.59 包相同的方案）。私钥 PEM 由项目负责人保管，不在代码仓库中（.gitignore 忽略 *.pem）。
     证书：CN=RhythmDesk Android Releases，SHA-256 指纹
     7E:CB:F5:86:D6:AD:73:D4:2D:A0:6C:77:3C:58:6F:E1:38:4E:4A:B3:46:D9:CC:6C:AD:CE:34:77:69:A4:B7:4F
     与 1.59 原包一致，因此新版可直接覆盖安装。tools/verify_v2.py 独立校验 v2 签名、内容摘要和对齐。

3. 构建命令
   python3 RhythmDesk-Win11/source/build_ui.py
   python3 tools/patch164.py RhythmDesk-Win11/source/ui android/index.html      （在 1.63 源码上应用 1.64 修改，已应用过的不要重复执行）
   python3 tools/repack_apk.py 上一版.apk android/index.html unsigned.apk 1.64
   java --add-exports java.base/sun.security.x509=ALL-UNNAMED --add-exports java.base/sun.security.pkcs=ALL-UNNAMED \
        --add-exports java.base/sun.security.util=ALL-UNNAMED -cp apksig-2.3.0.jar tools/SignApk.java 签名.pem unsigned.apk RhythmDesk-Android-1.64.apk
   python3 tools/verify_v2.py RhythmDesk-Android-1.64.apk

4. 测试（RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests）
   node interaction164.cjs                                  双击计时、滚轮缩放、拖动、保存流程、诊断文件版本
   node playback164.cjs 安卓index.html ../../RhythmDesk.html  安卓页面与浏览器页面播放跟随轨迹一致、循环释放规则
   node offline164.cjs 解压后的iPhone离线包目录                 iPhone 离线版 service worker（需要先生成 iPhone 包）
   node editor160.e2e.cjs 页面...                            （需 Playwright + Chromium）1.60 三项功能：复制轨道、带音符切分、长按菜单（1.63 起为 0.5 秒）
   node editor161.e2e.cjs 页面...                            （需 Playwright + Chromium）1.61/1.62 功能：再循环次数（自由输入）、竖向蓝色播放方框、音符锁定
   node editor163.e2e.cjs 页面...                            （需 Playwright + Chromium）1.63 功能：每个循环预设次数、0.5 秒长按、双击菜单避让
   node editor164.e2e.cjs 页面...                            （需 Playwright + Chromium）1.64：多选半透明选框（按像素检查竖线与音符可见）
   1.64 发布前上述测试（offline 除外）在 Win11 页面和从已签名 APK 中取出的页面上全部通过；未在安卓真机和 Windows Edge 上实测。

5. 版本记录
   1.59  接手版本（2026-11-17）。安卓 versionCode 159。
   1.60  2026-11-21：复制本轨道；写入音符状态下直接切分；双击菜单支持长按 1 秒并避开按压位置。versionCode 160。
   1.61  2026-11-22：循环「再循环 N 次后继续」按钮；所有轨道蓝色方框显示播放位置；长按音符键锁定点按输入。versionCode 161。
   1.62  2026-11-22：再循环次数自由输入（1–999）；播放位置改为竖向蓝色长方框覆盖符杆；说明文件核对同步；修复剩余次数不刷新。versionCode 162。
   1.63  2026-11-22：每个循环区间预设循环次数并自动继续；所有长按改为 0.5 秒；双击打开的菜单避开点按位置；说明书同步。versionCode 163。
   1.64  2026-11-22：多选模式中单个选中的符杆改为半透明蓝框（粘贴覆盖预览同样半透明）。versionCode 164。

==============================================================================
二、提示词汇总
==============================================================================
第 1 段提示词（初次接手，产出 1.60）：
@"RhythmDesk.zip" 该压缩包内有两个平台的软件版本，一个是win11版，一个是安卓版，其中安卓版的签名文件也已经在压缩包中，请按照要求修改基础操作功能，并同步到这两个平台的后续版本中，优化：在乐器轨道菜单里加入复制本轨道选项，复制内容为包括音符在内的所有内容，编辑乐谱时，切分按钮不需要清除音符再修改，可以在写入音符状态下直接修改，所有需要双击激活的菜单控制栏也可以同时使用长按激活，长按激活的时长是1秒，激活后的菜单要避开长按位置防止错按选项

  拆分为三项需求：
  需求 1：乐器轨道菜单加入「复制本轨道」，复制包括音符在内的所有内容。
  需求 2：编辑乐谱时切分按钮不需要先清除音符，在写入音符状态下直接修改。
  需求 3：所有需要双击激活的菜单也可长按激活，长按 1 秒，激活后的菜单避开长按位置防止误按。
  另：同步到 Win11 与安卓两个平台的后续版本（1.60），安卓用压缩包中的签名文件签名。

第 2 段提示词（产出 1.61 和本文档）：
在进行一次优化，此次优化内容如下：1. 在循环区间添加，点击方块向后继续播放时，再次循环几次的按钮功能。2. 播放时所有轨道都要显示音符播放的位置，显示方式改成一个蓝色方块阔到音符上面。3. 在编辑菜单音符按钮位置，可以选择长按该音符按钮，切换为锁定该音符点按进行谱子输入该音符模式。4.此次优化后，将所有优化内容整理汇总成一个文本文档作为交接给其他程序开发人员使用的参考，交接内容为从你初次接手该软件优化到这次优化为止的所有优化提示词汇总及每段提示词你都做了何种程序修改，要列出修改前那段位置的源程序及修改过后同样位置的程序代码对比。

  拆分为四项需求：
  需求 1：循环区间中按方块向后继续播放时，增加「再循环几次」的按钮功能。
  需求 2：播放时所有轨道显示音符播放位置，显示方式改为蓝色方框框住音符。
  需求 3：音符键盘上长按音符键，切换为锁定该音符、点按谱面输入该音符的模式。
  需求 4：整理本交接文档（本文件）。

第 3 段提示词（产出 1.62，并更新本文档）：
功能完善的很好，有3个小修改：1.循环次数可以自由填写，不用设定成固定次数。2.核对修改后的说明文件，有不同同步更新。3.播放时跟随显示的蓝色方框要竖的长方形，覆盖住竖线部分

  拆分为三项需求：
  需求 1：循环次数可以自由填写，不用设定成固定次数。
  需求 2：核对修改后的说明文件，不一致处同步更新。
  需求 3：播放时跟随显示的蓝色方框改为竖向长方形，覆盖住竖线（符杆）部分。

第 4 段提示词（产出 1.63，并延续本文档）：
循环设定可以在每个循环区域预设，如同一轨道出现多个循环，则可以在每个循环预设循环次数自动向后播放，所有长按激活的菜单长按激活时长改成0.5秒，另外双击激活的模式弹出菜单也要让开点击位置避免误点，同步说明书修正及延续交接内容

  拆分为四项需求：
  需求 1：每个循环区域可预设循环次数；同一轨道有多个循环时各自预设，播完自动向后播放。
  需求 2：所有长按激活的菜单，长按时长改为 0.5 秒。
  需求 3：双击激活弹出的菜单也要让开点击位置，避免误点。
  需求 4：同步修正说明书，并延续交接文档。

第 5 段提示词（产出 1.64，并延续本文档）：
多选模式下，单个点选音符竖线的蓝色选框要改成半透明，可以看到竖线及音符的谱面状态

  需求 1：多选模式下，单个点选符杆时的蓝色选框改为半透明，能看到竖线和音符的谱面状态。
  （按惯例同步说明书并延续本交接文档。）

==============================================================================
三、第 1 段提示词的程序修改（1.59 → 1.60）
==============================================================================
说明：「修改前」取自 1.59 原文件，「修改后」取自 1.60 文件，行号为各自文件中的行号；路径相对于 RhythmDesk-Win11/。
本轮补丁清单：tools/patch160.py（52 处修改）。

==============================================================================
需求 1：轨道菜单「复制本轨道」（1.59 → 1.60）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  在轨道标题「⋯」菜单的「↓ 下移轨道」之后加入「复制本轨道」。
  · model.js 新增 duplicateTrack(doc, track, name)：structuredClone 整条轨道（音符 cells、左右手 hands、
    measures 小节／拍号／切分、tempoRatio 速度、loops 循环、lineBreaks 换行、samples 自定义音色、preset、
    音量／声像／静音／独奏），分配新的唯一 id（t1、t2…），名称加「副本」（重名时加序号「副本 2」…，28 字以内），
    插入到原轨道下方；若原轨道属于 Bell+Drum 配对组，则插入到整个配对组下方。复制出来的 Bell 设为
    bellFor = null（不与任何鼓配对），原配对保持不变。
  · app.js 新增 duplicateTrackWithContent(source)：检查 100 条轨道上限和 640 KiB 采样上限（超出则提示），
    通过 change(...) 执行（可一次撤销），光标移到副本同一位置并滚动显示，状态栏提示。
  · app.js 新增 duplicateTrackName(name)：生成「原名 副本」/「原名 副本 2」…。
  · i18n.js 新增 track.duplicate / track.copySuffix / track.duplicated / track.duplicateLimit /
    track.duplicateBudget（中英文）；说明书「轨道标题「⋯」」条目补充说明。

本需求共 7 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

修改 1：source/ui/model.js
  安卓 assets/index.html：修改前 第 2081 行；修改后 第 2088–2104 行
【修改前 1.59】第 344 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
344 | function detachTrackPair(doc, track) {
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
【修改后 1.60】第 344–360 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
344 | // 1.60: an independent copy of one track with all of its content (notes, sticking, measures, group
345 | // meters, split cells, tempo, loops, line breaks, sounds, preset and mix), placed directly below the
346 | // track or below its Bell/Drum pair. A copied Bell is not paired with any drum.
347 | function duplicateTrack(doc, track, name) {
348 |   if (doc.tracks.length >= TRACK_LIMIT || !doc.tracks.includes(track)) return null;
349 |   const copy = structuredClone(track);
350 |   let number = 1;
351 |   while (doc.tracks.some(item => item.id === `t${number}`)) number++;
352 |   copy.id = `t${number}`;
353 |   copy.name = shortText(name, 28, track.name);
354 |   if (copy.type === 'bell') copy.bellFor = null;
355 |   const block = trackBlocks(doc).find(items => items.includes(track));
356 |   doc.tracks.splice(doc.tracks.indexOf(block.at(-1)) + 1, 0, copy);
357 |   return copy;
358 | }
359 |

【修改后 1.60】第 2510–2537 行

2510 | // 1.60: copy a track with everything in it (notes included) into a new track directly below.
2511 | function duplicateTrackWithContent(source) {
2512 |   const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === source.id);
2513 |   if (!track) return;
2514 |   if (doc.tracks.length >= TRACK_LIMIT) { notify(t('track.duplicateLimit')); return; }
2515 |   const extra = Object.values(track.samples).reduce((size, sample) => size + sampleBytes(sample), 0);
2516 |   if (projectSampleBytes(doc) + extra > SAMPLE_BUDGET) { notify(t('track.duplicateBudget')); return; }
2517 |   const position = track.id === cursor.track ? {beat: cursor.beat, slot: cursor.slot} : {beat: 0, slot: 0};
2518 |   let copy;
2519 |   change(() => {
2520 |     copy = duplicateTrack(doc, track, duplicateTrackName(track.name));
2521 |     cursor = {track: copy.id, ...position};
2522 |   }, true);
2523 |   revealSlot(scoreIndex(), copy.id);
2524 |   notify(t('track.duplicated', {track: track.name, copy: copy.name}));
2525 | }
2526 | 
2527 | function duplicateTrackName(name) {
2528 |   const suffix = t('track.copySuffix');
2529 |   const taken = new Set(doc.tracks.map(track => track.name));
2530 |   for (let number = 1; ; number++) {
2531 |     const ending = suffix + (number > 1 ? ' ' + number : '');
2532 |     const candidate = (name.slice(0, Math.max(0, 28 - ending.length)).trimEnd() + ending).trim();
2533 |     if (!taken.has(candidate)) return candidate;
2534 |   }
2535 | }
2536 |

==============================================================================
需求 2：写入音符状态下直接切分（1.59 → 1.60）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  切分（♫ 键、数字键 1–4、拍菜单「拆分为…」「切换 3/4 细分」）在已写入音符时直接修改，不再要求先清空。
  · app.js 删除 blockSubdivisionChange()（原先发现蓝框内有音符就弹出「请先清空蓝框内的音符，再更换切分小拍」），
    并去掉 changeTuplet / openSplitMenu / splitBeat 中对它的调用。
  · model.js splitTrackBeat()：被切分的那一格内，旧子格 i 映射到新子格 round(i × 新份数 / 旧份数)，
    其他格位置不变；只有两个音符落到同一格时才计为「重叠」（dropped），由调用方确认后合并。
    原先的规则是「不在新格位上的音符直接删除」。
  · 同时放宽了同类的批量细分（超出提示词字面范围、属于同一类操作）：applyBeatSizes（整谱「所有拍组改为 3/4 细分」）
    去掉「请先清空受影响小节」的拦截；openBulkSplit（小节／轨道／整谱「统一拆分」）去掉「请先清空所选范围」的拦截；
    model.js splitTrackRange() 不再抛错，改为返回 {track, merged}，重叠数交给 confirmBeatSizes 确认。
  · i18n.js 删除不再使用的 bulk.clearFirst / beat.clearBeforeSplit / beat.clearBeforeBulkSplit，
    beat.confirmSplitMerge 改为「重叠并被合并」的说法；说明书相应条目更新。拍号更换（meter）的「先清空」规则未改。

本需求共 21 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

1509 | function applyBeatSizes(sizes, subdivision) {
1510 |   let occupied = false;
1511 |   const edits = doc.tracks.map(track => {
1512 |     const grid = trackGrid(doc, track);
1513 |     const next = track.measures ? grid.beats.map(() => subdivision) : sizes;
1514 |     let offset = 0;
1515 |     grid.beats.forEach((size, beat) => {
1516 |       const end = offset + groupSize(size);
1517 |       if (size !== next[beat] && track.cells.slice(offset, end).some(Boolean)) occupied = true;
1518 |       offset = end;
1519 |     });
1520 |     const edit = convertCells(track.cells, grid.beats, next);
1521 |     if (track.hands) edit.collisions += convertCells(track.hands, grid.beats, next).collisions;
1522 |     return {track, grid, next, ...edit};
1523 |   });
1524 |   if (occupied) { warnBeforeChangingNotes('beat.clearBeforeBulkSplit'); return; }
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

==============================================================================
需求 3：双击菜单同时支持长按 1 秒，菜单避开长按位置（1.59 → 1.60）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  所有需要双击打开的菜单同时支持按住 1 秒打开（手指、触控笔、鼠标左键均可），菜单避开按压位置。
  · 适用控件：符杆（拍菜单）、符杆上方蓝点（左右手菜单）、小节线（小节菜单）、组拍号（拍号菜单）、组速度（速度弹窗）。
  · app.js 新增 bindMenuHold(node, open, allowed)：pointerdown 起计时 1000 ms；移动超过 10 px、第二根手指按下、
    pointerup/pointercancel、滚轮、窗口失焦都会取消；触发时记录按压点 menuHoldPoint；松手后 400 ms 内的 click 被吞掉，
    所以抬手不会点中菜单项，也不会触发原控件的单击；同时阻止这些控件的系统 contextmenu。
  · app.js 新增 clearOfMenuHold(box, bounds)：若菜单矩形（外扩 40 px）覆盖按压点，依次尝试放到按压点上方、下方、
    右侧、左侧及四个斜角；都放不下时选重叠最少的位置并标记 covered。新增 menuHoldRoom(bounds)。
  · positionPopup()（小节线／拍号／左右手菜单）：covered 时把可滚动菜单的 max-height 缩到按压点上下较大一侧的空间。
    positionBeatMenu()（拍菜单）：移动后加 clear-of-hold 类以隐藏指向箭头。openTempoEditor()（速度弹窗）：打开后若遮挡按压点则改为固定定位移开。
  · 多选模式下符杆保持原来的 0.5 秒长按选整组规则，不触发 1 秒菜单。
  · style.css：这些控件禁止文字选择和系统长按菜单；.beat-menu.clear-of-hold::after 隐藏箭头。
  · 开发中的调整：① 菜单尺寸为整数像素，摆放时多留 2 px 余量；② 手机上很长的小节线菜单上下都放不下时缩短并可滚动；
    ③ 去掉「滚动即取消长按」，因为播放跟随会滚动谱面，导致鼠标长按在 0.9 秒后被打断（只保留滚轮取消）。

本需求共 22 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

137 | // 1.60: every control that opens a menu on a double-tap also opens it when held for one second
138 | // (finger, pen or left mouse button). The menu then keeps clear of the held point, and the click
139 | // that ends the hold is dropped, so lifting the finger never chooses a menu item.
140 | const MENU_HOLD_MS = 1000;
141 | const MENU_HOLD_SLOP = 10;
142 | const MENU_HOLD_CLEARANCE = 40;
143 | let menuHold = null;
144 | let menuHoldPoint = null;
145 | let menuHoldClickUntil = 0;
146 | function cancelMenuHold() {
147 |   if (menuHold) clearTimeout(menuHold.timer);
148 |   menuHold = null;
149 | }
150 | function bindMenuHold(node, open, allowed = () => true) {
151 |   node.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
152 |     cancelMenuHold();
153 |     if (!event.isPrimary || event.button !== 0 || !allowed()) return;
154 |     const press = {node, id: event.pointerId, x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY, target: event.target};
155 |     press.timer = setTimeout(() => {
156 |       if (menuHold !== press) return;
157 |       menuHold = null;
158 |       if (!node.isConnected || !allowed()) return;
159 |       quickTap = null;
160 |       menuHoldPoint = {id: press.id, x: press.x, y: press.y};
161 |       menuHoldClickUntil = Infinity;
162 |       open(press);
163 |     }, MENU_HOLD_MS);
164 |     menuHold = press;
165 |   });
166 |   node.addEventListener('contextmenu', event => event.preventDefault());
167 | }
168 | document.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
169 |   // A second finger (pinch) or another pointer ends the hold.
170 |   if (menuHold && menuHold.id !== event.pointerId) cancelMenuHold();
171 | }, true);
172 | document.addEventListener('pointermove', event => {
173 |   if (menuHold?.id === event.pointerId &&
174 |       Math.hypot(event.clientX - menuHold.x, event.clientY - menuHold.y) > MENU_HOLD_SLOP) cancelMenuHold();
175 | }, true);
176 | for (const name of ['pointerup', 'pointercancel']) {
177 |   document.addEventListener(name, event => {
178 |     if (menuHold?.id === event.pointerId) cancelMenuHold();
179 |     if (menuHoldPoint?.id !== event.pointerId) return;
180 |     menuHoldPoint = null;
181 |     menuHoldClickUntil = performance.now() + 400;
182 |   }, true);
183 | }
184 | window.addEventListener('click', event => {
185 |   if (performance.now() >= menuHoldClickUntil) return;
186 |   menuHoldClickUntil = 0;
187 |   event.preventDefault();
188 |   event.stopImmediatePropagation();
189 | }, true);
190 | // Wheel zoom moves the score under a held mouse. Programmatic scrolling (playback following) does not
191 | // cancel a hold; finger panning already ends it with pointercancel.
192 | window.addEventListener('wheel', cancelMenuHold, {capture: true, passive: true});
193 | window.addEventListener('blur', cancelMenuHold);
194 | 
195 | // Move a menu box {left, top, width, height} so it does not cover the point still held by a long press.
196 | // Returns the same object when no move is needed.
197 | function clearOfMenuHold(box, bounds) {
198 |   const point = menuHoldPoint;
199 |   if (!point) return box;
200 |   const reach = MENU_HOLD_CLEARANCE;
201 |   const covers = ({left, top}) => point.x > left - reach && point.x < left + box.width + reach &&
202 |     point.y > top - reach && point.y < top + box.height + reach;
203 |   if (!covers(box)) return box;
204 |   const fit = (left, top) => ({...box,
205 |     left: clamp(left, bounds.left, Math.max(bounds.left, bounds.right - box.width)),
206 |     top: clamp(top, bounds.top, Math.max(bounds.top, bounds.bottom - box.height))});
207 |   // Layout sizes are rounded to whole pixels, so place the menu with a small margin beyond the reach.
208 |   const gap = reach + 2;
209 |   const aboveTop = point.y - gap - box.height, belowTop = point.y + gap;
210 |   const rightLeft = point.x + gap, leftLeft = point.x - gap - box.width;
211 |   // Above the finger first: the hand usually covers the screen below the fingertip.
212 |   const options = [fit(box.left, aboveTop), fit(box.left, belowTop), fit(rightLeft, box.top), fit(leftLeft, box.top),
213 |     fit(rightLeft, aboveTop), fit(leftLeft, aboveTop), fit(rightLeft, belowTop), fit(leftLeft, belowTop)];
214 |   const clear = options.find(option => !covers(option));
215 |   if (clear) return clear;
216 |   // No free side is large enough: keep as much of the menu as possible away from the finger, and tell
217 |   // the caller so a scrollable menu can shorten itself to menuHoldRoom() and try again.
218 |   const overlap = option =>
219 |     Math.max(0, Math.min(point.x + reach, option.left + box.width) - Math.max(point.x - reach, option.left)) *
220 |     Math.max(0, Math.min(point.y + reach, option.top + box.height) - Math.max(point.y - reach, option.top));
221 |   return {...options.reduce((best, option) => overlap(option) < overlap(best) ? option : best), covered: true};
222 | }
223 | 
224 | // The larger free height above or below the held point.
225 | function menuHoldRoom(bounds) {
226 |   const point = menuHoldPoint;
227 |   if (!point) return Infinity;
228 |   const gap = MENU_HOLD_CLEARANCE + 2;
229 |   return Math.max(point.y - gap - bounds.top, bounds.bottom - point.y - gap);
230 | }
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

【修改后 1.60】第 1470–1488 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1470 | function positionPopup(anchor, handMenu = false) {
1471 |   const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
1472 |   const bounds = {left: 7, top: 6, right: innerWidth - 7, bottom: innerHeight - 7};
1473 |   const place = () => {
1474 |     const width = el.menu.offsetWidth, height = el.menu.offsetHeight;
1475 |     const left = clamp(rect.left, 7, innerWidth - width - 7);
1476 |     const below = handMenu ? rect.top + Math.min(rect.height * .19, 28) + 4 : rect.bottom + 4;
1477 |     return clearOfMenuHold({left, top: clamp(below, 6, innerHeight - height - 7), width, height}, bounds);
1478 |   };
1479 |   el.menu.style.maxHeight = '';
1480 |   let box = place();
1481 |   if (box.covered) {
1482 |     // Too tall for the space beside a held finger: shorten the scrollable menu to the larger free side.
1483 |     el.menu.style.maxHeight = Math.max(48, Math.floor(menuHoldRoom(bounds))) + 'px';
1484 |     box = place();
1485 |   }
1486 |   el.menu.style.left = box.left + 'px';
1487 |   el.menu.style.top = box.top + 'px';
1488 | }

修改 48：source/ui/help/guide.en.html

==============================================================================
四、第 2 段提示词的程序修改（1.60 → 1.61）
==============================================================================
说明：「修改前」取自 1.60 文件，「修改后」取自 1.61 文件，行号为各自文件中的行号；路径相对于 RhythmDesk-Win11/。
本轮补丁清单：tools/patch161.py（40 处修改）。

==============================================================================
需求 1：循环区间「再循环几次后继续」按钮（1.60 → 1.61）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  循环区间播放时，停止方块上方出现「↻ 再循环…」按钮，可选择再循环 1–8 次后自动向后继续。
  · audio.js 新增 gateLoopsBack(gate)：循环终点在「未释放」或「已释放但仍有剩余次数 repeats > 0」时回到起点。
    PlaybackCursor 的每个 gate 增加 repeats 字段；take() 在已释放的 gate 回环时 repeats--；waitingAtBoundary、
    playheadGroupMotion().loopEnd、playheadMotion() 改用 gateLoopsBack。
  · audio.js releaseLoop(repeats = 0)：释放当前循环并设定额外次数（方块按钮仍是 0 = 播完本轮继续）；
    新增 keepLooping()：撤回待执行的「继续」，恢复一直循环。
  · app.js：引擎回调的循环状态串加入 repeats（剩余次数变化时刷新按钮）；refreshControls() 计算剩余次数，
    停止按钮提示改为「将再循环 N 次后继续播放」，并显示／隐藏、定位 #loopRepeat 按钮（文字「↻ 再循环…」或「↻ 再循环 N 次」）；
    followLimit() 改用 gateLoopsBack（跟随镜头把仍会回环的终点当作边界）。
  · app.js 新增 openLoopRepeatMenu(anchor)（菜单：再循环 1–8 次后继续、取消继续保持循环）和 changeLoopRelease(times)
    （调用引擎、重算跟随镜头、提示）；该菜单带 transport-menu 类，播放跟随滚动谱面时不会被关闭；循环结束时自动关闭。
  · index.html 在 #stop 后加入 #loopRepeat 按钮；style.css 让 .transport-inner 相对定位并定义按钮样式（剩余次数时为蓝底）。
  · 边界规则与方块按钮一致：在一轮的最后约 0.12 秒内（调度器已提交回环）做选择，从下一轮开始计数。

本需求共 24 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

1982 | function openLoopRepeatMenu(anchor) {
1983 |   const gates = engine.paused ? [] : playingLoops;
1984 |   if (!gates.length) return;
1985 |   const released = gates.every(gate => gate.released);
1986 |   const left = Math.max(0, ...gates.map(gate => gate.released ? gate.repeats || 0 : 0));
1987 |   popup(anchor, [
1988 |     ...[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map(times =>
1989 |       [(released && left === times ? '✓ ' : '') + t('loop.repeatTimes', {n: times}), () => changeLoopRelease(times)]),
1990 |     [t('loop.keepLooping'), () => changeLoopRelease(null), !released]
1991 |   ]);
1992 |   el.menu.classList.add('transport-menu');
1993 | }
1994 | 
1995 | function changeLoopRelease(times) {
1996 |   if (!(times === null ? engine.keepLooping() : engine.releaseLoop(times))) return;
1997 |   if (canFollow()) {
1998 |     // Re-plan the camera for the new loop end, as the square button does.
1999 |     stopFollowing('release');
2000 |     animateFollow(performance.now());
2001 |   }
2002 |   refreshControls();
2003 |   notify(times === null ? t('loop.keptLooping') : t('loop.repeatSet', {n: times}));
2004 | }

==============================================================================
需求 2：所有轨道用蓝色方框标出正在播放的音符（1.60 → 1.61）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  播放时每条轨道都用蓝色方框框住正在播放的音符（休止处框住该位置）。
  · 播放位置原本由 paintPlaySteps() 给每条轨道当前格加 .playing 类（各轨道本来就都有标记，但只是一条 2 px 细竖线）。
  · style.css：.slot.playing::after 由 2 px 竖线改为 28×28 px 方框（3 px 蓝色边框、半透明蓝底、圆角），
    垂直位置对准音符头（top: calc(72% - 14px)），切分很密时宽度不超过该格宽度 + 6 px。方框随谱面缩放。
  · 暂停时方框保留，停止后清除（沿用原逻辑）。说明书新增「播放位置」条目。

本需求共 3 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

修改 25：source/ui/style.css
  安卓 assets/index.html：修改前 第 118 行；修改后 第 118–119 行

64 |     <dt>Follow and panning</dt><dd>Follow initially uses the first track. Selecting a stem designates that track as the follow target; other tracks’ loops or endings do not change it. Starting playback first positions the selected track. Pausing preserves the displayed position, and resuming continues from there. Horizontal movement begins when the playhead reaches about 70% of the viewport width and follows each group’s actual playback duration, rather than restarting at each note, rest or subdivision. Entry into a later loop stays continuous; a loop return instantly aligns its start, then following continues at the same threshold. Line wraps position the view on the appropriate row. Ordinary horizontal following preserves your vertical position and supports tempo changes and zoom from 50% to 500% during playback. Dragging takes priority; after release, horizontal following resumes at your chosen vertical position. Tap Follow again to turn it off.</dd>

==============================================================================
需求 3：长按音符键锁定该音符，点按谱面连续输入（1.60 → 1.61）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  长按音符键（或「休止」）1 秒锁定该键，之后点按谱面符杆即在该位置输入该音符。
  · app.js 新增状态 noteLock = {stroke, type}（stroke 为 null 表示锁定「休止」）。
  · updateKeyboard()：鼓音键和「休止」键改用 lockableKey(...) 生成（data-stroke 标记、提示文字、bindMenuHold 1 秒长按切换锁定），
    单击改为 tapNoteKey(stroke)：点被锁定的键＝解锁，点其他键＝原来的写入并前进；键盘重建后 paintNoteLock() 恢复锁定样式；
    键盘收起（keysOpen = false）时自动解锁。
  · 新增 setNoteLock / paintNoteLock / unlockNote / toggleNoteLock / tapNoteKey / writeLockedStroke。
    writeLockedStroke()：在点中的位置写入锁定音符、光标不前进；该位置已是同一音符则清除；锁定「休止」则清除；
    点到不含该音符的乐器（如 Kenkeni 上锁定了低音）只选中并提示。
  · 符杆单击处理：锁定时单击＝选中并 writeLockedStroke()，不再用双击打开拍菜单（按住 1 秒仍可打开拍菜单）；蓝点（左右手）不受影响。
  · Esc 先解锁；长按另一个键改锁该键。style.css：.key.locked 蓝底白字带白点，锁定时谱面光标为 copy。

本需求共 11 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

【修改后 1.61】第 899–954 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
899 |     lockableKey(key(t('keys.rest'), () => tapNoteKey(null), 'silence'), null), key('←', () => moveCursor(-1), 'aux'), key('→', () => moveCursor(1), 'aux'));
900 |   el.keys.replaceChildren(...rows, main, nav);
901 |   paintNoteLock();
902 | }
903 | 
904 | // 1.61: hold a note key (or Rest) for 1 s to lock it. While locked, tapping a stem enters that note
905 | // there without moving on (tapping the same note again clears it; a locked Rest clears). A tap on
906 | // the locked key or Esc unlocks; holding another key moves the lock.
907 | function lockableKey(node, stroke) {
908 |   node.dataset.stroke = stroke ?? '';
909 |   if (!stroke) node.title = t('keys.rest') + ' · ' + t('keys.lockHint');
910 |   bindMenuHold(node, () => toggleNoteLock(stroke), () => !multiGroups);
911 |   return node;
912 | }
913 | 
914 | function setNoteLock(stroke) {
915 |   noteLock = stroke === undefined ? undefined
916 |     : {stroke, type: stroke ? Object.keys(TYPES).find(type => TYPES[type].strokes.includes(stroke)) : null};
917 |   if (noteLock?.stroke && TYPES[selectedTrack().type].strokes.includes(noteLock.stroke)) noteLock.type = selectedTrack().type;
918 |   paintNoteLock();
919 | }
920 | 
921 | function paintNoteLock() {
922 |   for (const node of el.keys.querySelectorAll('.key[data-stroke]')) {
923 |     const locked = Boolean(noteLock) && node.dataset.stroke === (noteLock.stroke ?? '');
924 |     node.classList.toggle('locked', locked);
925 |     node.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(locked));
926 |   }
927 |   el.paper.classList.toggle('note-lock', Boolean(noteLock));
928 | }
929 | 
930 | function unlockNote() {
931 |   setNoteLock();
932 |   notify(t('lock.off'));
933 | }
934 | 
935 | function toggleNoteLock(stroke) {
936 |   if (noteLock && noteLock.stroke === stroke) { unlockNote(); return; }
937 |   setNoteLock(stroke);
938 |   notify(stroke ? t('lock.on', {note: noteLabel(stroke, noteLock.type)}) : t('lock.onRest'));
939 | }
940 | 
941 | function tapNoteKey(stroke) {
942 |   if (noteLock && noteLock.stroke === stroke) unlockNote();
943 |   else writeStroke(stroke);
944 | }
945 | 
946 | function writeLockedStroke() {
947 |   const track = selectedTrack();
948 |   const {stroke, type} = noteLock;
949 |   if (stroke && !TYPES[track.type].strokes.includes(stroke)) {
950 |     notify(t('lock.unsupported', {track: track.name, note: noteLabel(stroke, type)}));
951 |     return;
952 |   }
953 |   writeStroke(stroke && track.cells[scoreIndex()] === stroke ? null : stroke, 0);
954 | }
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

26 |     <dt>Stroke keys</dt><dd>Djembe offers B (bass), T (tone), S (slap X), H (alternate high x), G (muted slap), P (light touch) and BT, BS, TT, TS, ST, SS, TB, HB grace combinations in playing order. Dununba, Sangba and Kenkeni use O (open), M (muted), K (stick on rim); Bell and Shekere use X. Hold any stroke key or Rest for 1 second to lock it (the key turns blue): tapping a stem then enters that note there without moving on; tap the same stem again to clear it, and a locked Rest clears what you tap. While locked, a double-tap no longer opens the beat menu, but a 1-second hold on a stem still does. Tap the locked key or press Esc to unlock; hold another key to move the lock.</dd>

■ 新增文件（修改前不存在）
  RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests/editor161.e2e.cjs  —— 1.61 功能的 Playwright 测试，全文见附录 B。
  tools/patch161.py、tools/make_handoff.py（生成本文档）；tools/repack_apk.py 改为通用版本参数。

■ 本轮开发中的注意点
  · 版本号替换只替换「1.60 ·」「'1.60'」「-1.60.json」形式的版本字符串：代码注释「// 1.60: …」保留引入时的版本；
    audio.js 的音色合成表里有一个常数 1.60（dundunba:M 参数，原始数据），不能被当成版本号替换。
  · gateLoopsBack 写成函数声明并放在 PlaybackCursor 类之后，因为 playback161.cjs 按「class PlaybackCursor { … async function exportBacking(」
    截取代码执行；函数声明会提升，在拼装后的页面里位置不影响使用。
  · 音符锁定复用了 1.60 的 bindMenuHold（1 秒、移动取消、松手不触发单击），与菜单长按的手感一致。

==============================================================================
五、第 3 段提示词的程序修改（1.61 → 1.62）
==============================================================================
说明：「修改前」取自 1.61 文件，「修改后」取自 1.62 文件，行号为各自文件中的行号；路径相对于 RhythmDesk-Win11/。
本轮补丁清单：tools/patch162.py（24 处修改）。

==============================================================================
需求 1：循环次数自由填写（取代固定的 1–8 次菜单）（1.61 → 1.62）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  循环次数改为自由填写，取代 1.61 的固定 1–8 次菜单。
  · app.js：openLoopRepeatMenu（1–8 次菜单）替换为 openLoopRepeatDialog()。点「↻ 再循环…」打开与速度弹窗同样式的对话框：
    数字输入框（1–999；默认显示当前剩余次数，没有待执行的继续时为 1；打开后全选，便于直接输入），−／＋ 微调，
    「完成」或回车确认，「取消」不作改动；已设定继续时多出「取消继续，保持循环」。非整数、小于 1 或大于 999 时提示并保持弹窗。
    新增常量 LOOP_REPEAT_MAX = 999。弹窗打开期间播放继续进行。
  · changeLoopRelease()：若弹窗打开期间循环已经结束，提示「循环已经结束，未作更改」。refreshControls()：循环结束或暂停时自动关闭次数弹窗。
  · 去掉 1.61 为弹出菜单加的 transport-menu 相关代码（closePopups、谱面滚动处理、refreshControls 三处），因为不再使用弹出菜单。
  · 修复 1.61 遗留问题：方块上方按钮和停止按钮提示里的剩余次数不会逐次递减。原因是循环状态对象在调度时原地 repeats--，
    回调里用同一批对象重新计算「旧状态」，与新状态永远相同，所以不刷新。新增 playingLoopState 保存上次显示的状态串并用它比较，
    stopPlayback() 时清空；测试 playback162.cjs 的沙箱同步预置 playingLoopState。
  · i18n.js：删除 loop.repeatTimes，新增 loop.repeatTitle / loop.repeatHint / loop.repeatGone（中英文）；style.css 新增
    .loop-repeat-hint、.loop-keep；说明书两处「1–8 次」改为自由输入的说明。

本需求共 16 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

修改 1：source/ui/app.js
  安卓 assets/index.html：修改前 第 6187–6199 行；修改后 第 6198–6234 行
【修改前 1.61】第 1981–1993 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1981 | // 1.61: repeat the playing loop 1-8 more times, then continue; or cancel a pending continue.
1982 | function openLoopRepeatMenu(anchor) {
1983 |   const gates = engine.paused ? [] : playingLoops;
1984 |   if (!gates.length) return;
1985 |   const released = gates.every(gate => gate.released);
1986 |   const left = Math.max(0, ...gates.map(gate => gate.released ? gate.repeats || 0 : 0));
1987 |   popup(anchor, [
1988 |     ...[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8].map(times =>
1989 |       [(released && left === times ? '✓ ' : '') + t('loop.repeatTimes', {n: times}), () => changeLoopRelease(times)]),
1990 |     [t('loop.keepLooping'), () => changeLoopRelease(null), !released]
1991 |   ]);
1992 |   el.menu.classList.add('transport-menu');
1993 | }
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
【修改后 1.62】第 1984–2020 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1984 | // 1.62: type how many more passes (1-999) the playing loop plays before continuing, instead of
1985 | // choosing from a fixed list; the same box can cancel a pending continue. Playback keeps running.
1986 | const LOOP_REPEAT_MAX = 999;
1987 | function openLoopRepeatDialog() {
1988 |   const gates = engine.paused ? [] : playingLoops;
1989 |   if (!gates.length) return;
1990 |   closePopups();
1991 |   const released = gates.every(gate => gate.released);
1992 |   const left = Math.max(0, ...gates.map(gate => gate.released ? gate.repeats || 0 : 0));
1993 |   const panel = document.createElement('dialog'); panel.className = 'tempo-editor loop-repeat-editor';
1994 |   const form = document.createElement('form'); form.className = 'tempo-form';
1995 |   const title = document.createElement('h3'); title.textContent = t('loop.repeatTitle');
1996 |   const controls = document.createElement('div'); controls.className = 'tempo-controls';
1997 |   const input = document.createElement('input'); input.type = 'number'; input.min = 1; input.max = LOOP_REPEAT_MAX;
1998 |   input.step = 1; input.required = true; input.inputMode = 'numeric'; input.value = left || 1;
1999 |   input.setAttribute('aria-label', t('loop.repeatTitle'));
2000 |   const bump = n => { input.value = clamp(Math.round(Number(input.value) || 1) + n, 1, LOOP_REPEAT_MAX); };
2001 |   controls.append(button('−', () => bump(-1)), input, button('+', () => bump(1)));
2002 |   const hint = document.createElement('p'); hint.className = 'loop-repeat-hint'; hint.textContent = t('loop.repeatHint');
2003 |   const actions = document.createElement('div'); actions.className = 'tempo-actions';
2004 |   const confirm = document.createElement('button'); confirm.type = 'submit'; confirm.className = 'tempo-confirm';
2005 |   confirm.textContent = t('dialog.done');
2006 |   actions.append(button(t('dialog.cancel'), () => panel.close(), 'tempo-cancel'), confirm);
2007 |   form.append(title, controls, hint, actions);
2008 |   if (released) form.append(button(t('loop.keepLooping'), () => { panel.close(); changeLoopRelease(null); }, 'loop-keep'));
2009 |   form.addEventListener('submit', event => {
2010 |     event.preventDefault();
2011 |     const times = Number(input.value);
2012 |     if (!Number.isInteger(times) || times < 1 || times > LOOP_REPEAT_MAX) { input.reportValidity(); return; }
2013 |     panel.close();
2014 |     changeLoopRelease(times);
2015 |   });
2016 |   panel.append(form);
2017 |   panel.addEventListener('close', () => panel.remove(), {once: true});
2018 |   document.body.append(panel); panel.showModal();
2019 |   input.select();
2020 | }
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Play starts from the edit cursor and toggles pause or resume. During a track loop, the square button releases the loop and lets playback continue; otherwise it stops. While a loop plays, a ↻ Repeat… chip above the square opens a box: type how many more times the loop plays (1–999, or use −/+), choose Done, and playback continues once those passes finish; the chip shows the passes left. During playback, the Stop square releases the active loop at the end of its current pass and continues into the following section; the ↻ Repeat… chip above it first plays the loop 1–8 more times. The score ⋯ menu offers Play Once and Loop Score; configure section loops in barline menus.</dd>

During playback, the Stop square releases the active loop at the end of its current pass and continues into the following section; the ↻ Repeat… chip above it lets you type how many more times (1–999) the loop plays first. ==============================================================================
需求 2：核对说明文件，不一致处同步更新（1.61 → 1.62）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  核对修改后的说明文件（中英文说明书、关于页、Readme、仓库 README、本交接文档），不一致处同步更新：
  · 说明书与关于页中「再循环 1–8 次」「蓝色方框」的描述按 1.62 行为改写（见需求 1、需求 3 的修改）。
  · 选中框与播放框都是蓝色，容易混淆：说明书中指选中框的「蓝框」改称「蓝色选框」（回到开头、跟随按钮两处）。
  · 英文说明书「Score and group meters」补上中文版已有的规定：更换组拍号前需先清空该组音符（软件行为未变，原英文漏写）。
  · Readme.txt 与 source/Readme-Win11.txt 首行版本改为 1.62 并新增「1.62 更新」；本交接文档加入第 3 段提示词与全部修改对照，
    并更新版本记录、构建与测试命令。（仓库 README.md 的 1.62 段落本应同时加入，但当时的更新命令因前一步比对失败而未执行，
    1.63 时发现并补上，见第 4 段提示词的需求 4。）
  · 其余说明（复制本轨道、带音符切分、长按 1 秒菜单、音符锁定、再循环的边界规则）已逐条对照软件行为，无需改动。

本需求共 3 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

72 |         <dt>跟随按钮与画面拖动</dt><dd>打开跟随后，默认跟随第一条轨道；点选符杆后，以蓝框所在轨道为跟随目标，不因其他轨道的循环或结束而切换。开始播放时先定位指定轨道；暂停保留画面位置，继续播放时从停留处衔接。光标到达画面宽度约 70% 的位置后开始横向跟随，按横线组的实际播放时长连续移动，空拍、细分和律动不会让画面逐音符起停。首次进入后续循环区间时保持连续跟随；循环回跳瞬间对齐该循环起点，随后在光标达到约 70% 时继续移动。换行时自动定位到相应行。普通横向跟随保留纵向位置，支持播放中调节速度和 50%–500% 缩放。手指或鼠标拖动优先，松开后在选定的纵向位置恢复横向跟随。再次点跟随按钮可关闭。</dd>

==============================================================================
需求 3：播放位置改为竖向蓝色长方框，覆盖符杆（1.61 → 1.62）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  播放位置标记由 1.61 的 28×28 px 方框改为竖向长方框，覆盖整根符杆和音头。
  · style.css .slot.playing::after：top 19%、bottom 5%（从横梁处到音头下方，约 45 px 高），宽 22 px（切分很密时不超过格宽 + 4 px），
    3 px 蓝色边框、半透明蓝底、圆角 5 px，水平中心对齐符杆（--stem-offset）；休止处同样罩住该位置的竖线。方框随谱面缩放。
  · 说明书「播放位置」条目改为竖向长方框的描述。

本需求共 3 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

■ 本轮开发中的注意点
  · 剩余次数不刷新的问题是在 1.62 截图核对时发现的（弹窗显示 11 次、按钮仍显示 12 次），已修复并加入测试：
    editor161.e2e.cjs 现在检查「再循环 2 次」后按钮会变为「再循环 1 次」，停止按钮提示同步变化。

==============================================================================
六、第 4 段提示词的程序修改（1.62 → 1.63）
==============================================================================
说明：「修改前」取自 1.62 文件，「修改后」取自 1.63 文件，行号为各自文件中的行号；路径相对于 RhythmDesk-Win11/。
本轮补丁清单：tools/patch163.py（56 处修改）。

==============================================================================
需求 1：每个循环区间预设循环次数，播完自动向后播放（1.62 → 1.63）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  每个循环区间可以预设循环次数，播放到该循环时播完预设遍数便自动向后播放；同一轨道的多个循环各自设定。
  · 次数的含义：该循环一共播放几遍（含第一遍，1–999）。不设次数（留空）＝与以前一样一直循环，直到按方块。
  · 数据：track.loops 的每一项可带 times 字段（如 {from: 0, to: 1, times: 3}），随 .rhythm.json 保存。model.js 新增常量
    LOOP_TIMES_MAX = 999；readLoops() 读取并校验 times（非 1–999 的整数一律忽略，视为一直循环）。旧版本软件读取新文件时
    会忽略 times（只读 from/to），即退回一直循环，不会报错。
  · 编辑小节时保留次数：deleteBar() 的 afterRemoval、insertTrackBarAt()（在第 1 小节前插入）、deleteTrackBar() 原来重建
    循环对象时只复制 from/to，改为 {...range, from, to}，避免插入／删除小节后丢失次数（shiftTrackLoopsAfterInsert 原地修改，无需改）。
  · 播放引擎（audio.js）：start() 把 times 一并传入 PlaybackCursor；restart() 创建 gate 时，有预设次数的循环直接
    released = true、repeats = times − 1，于是播完 times 遍自动继续。整曲循环（wrap）重新开始时会再次按预设执行。
    方块上方的「↻ 再循环…」和方块按钮仍可在本次播放中改写或取消（取消继续＝一直循环），不修改预设本身。
  · 界面（app.js）：小节边界菜单在「更改此循环终点」之后新增「设置结束于此的循环次数…（当前状态）」和
    「设置起始于此的循环次数…（当前状态）」；轨道「⋯」在「移除本小节的循环」之后新增「设置本小节循环次数…（当前状态）」。
    新增 loopTimesState()、openLoopTimesDialog()（与速度弹窗同样式：数字输入 1–999、−／＋、完成／取消、
    已设次数时显示「一直循环（清除次数）」；留空确认＝清除）、setLoopTimes()（按 id 与 from/to 找回当前循环，
    通过 change(…, true) 修改，可撤销，修改时停止播放，与其他循环编辑一致）。
  · 谱面标记：boundaryAt() 在设有次数的循环结束竖线左上方显示「×N」（.loop-times，17 px 粗体蓝字，随谱面缩放），
    并在该竖线的提示文字中加上「共 N 遍」。
  · i18n.js 新增 loop.timesIncoming / loop.timesOutgoing / track.loopTimes / loop.timesState / loop.timesUnset / loop.bars /
    loop.timesTitle / loop.timesHint / loop.timesClear / loop.timesSet / loop.timesCleared（中英文）；说明书「轨道循环与整曲循环」
    和「回到开头／播放／停止方块」条目补充说明。

本需求共 18 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

2871 | // 1.63: preset how many times a track loop plays in total (1-999, including the first pass) before
2872 | // playback continues by itself. Blank or "Loop until stopped" keeps looping until the square is pressed.
2873 | function loopTimesState(range) {
2874 |   return range.times ? t('loop.timesState', {n: range.times}) : t('loop.timesUnset');
2875 | }
2876 | 
2877 | function openLoopTimesDialog(source, range) {
2878 |   closePopups();
2879 |   const panel = document.createElement('dialog'); panel.className = 'tempo-editor loop-times-editor';
2880 |   const form = document.createElement('form'); form.className = 'tempo-form';
2881 |   const title = document.createElement('h3');
2882 |   const bars = range.to - range.from > 1 ? t('loop.bars', {from: range.from + 1, to: range.to}) : t('bar.measure', {bar: range.to});
2883 |   title.textContent = t('loop.timesTitle', {track: source.name, bars});
2884 |   const controls = document.createElement('div'); controls.className = 'tempo-controls';
2885 |   const input = document.createElement('input'); input.type = 'number'; input.min = 1; input.max = LOOP_TIMES_MAX;
2886 |   input.step = 1; input.inputMode = 'numeric'; input.value = range.times ?? ''; input.placeholder = t('loop.timesUnset');
2887 |   input.setAttribute('aria-label', title.textContent);
2888 |   const bump = n => { input.value = clamp(Math.round(Number(input.value) || 1) + n, 1, LOOP_TIMES_MAX); };
2889 |   controls.append(button('−', () => bump(-1)), input, button('+', () => bump(1)));
2890 |   const hint = document.createElement('p'); hint.className = 'loop-repeat-hint'; hint.textContent = t('loop.timesHint');
2891 |   const actions = document.createElement('div'); actions.className = 'tempo-actions';
2892 |   const confirm = document.createElement('button'); confirm.type = 'submit'; confirm.className = 'tempo-confirm';
2893 |   confirm.textContent = t('dialog.done');
2894 |   actions.append(button(t('dialog.cancel'), () => panel.close(), 'tempo-cancel'), confirm);
2895 |   form.append(title, controls, hint, actions);
2896 |   if (range.times) form.append(button(t('loop.timesClear'), () => { panel.close(); setLoopTimes(source, range, null); }, 'loop-keep'));
2897 |   form.addEventListener('submit', event => {
2898 |     event.preventDefault();
2899 |     if (input.value.trim() === '' && !input.validity.badInput) { panel.close(); setLoopTimes(source, range, null); return; }
2900 |     const times = Number(input.value);
2901 |     if (!Number.isInteger(times) || times < 1 || times > LOOP_TIMES_MAX) { input.reportValidity(); return; }
2902 |     panel.close();
2903 |     setLoopTimes(source, range, times);
2904 |   });
2905 |   panel.append(form);
2906 |   panel.addEventListener('close', () => panel.remove(), {once: true});
2907 |   document.body.append(panel); panel.showModal();
2908 |   input.select();
2909 | }
2910 | 
2911 | function setLoopTimes(source, range, times) {
2912 |   const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === source.id);
2913 |   const loop = track?.loops.find(item => item.from === range.from && item.to === range.to);
2914 |   if (!loop || (loop.times ?? null) === times) return;
2915 |   change(() => { if (times) loop.times = times; else delete loop.times; }, true);
2916 |   notify(times ? t('loop.timesSet', {n: times}) : t('loop.timesCleared'));
2917 | }

49 |         <dt>轨道循环与整曲循环</dt><dd>双击或长按 0.5 秒小节边界设置轨道循环起点与终点。终点须在起点之后，同轨循环不可重叠或越过下一个循环；已设终点可再次修改。每个循环都可预设循环次数：在小节边界菜单的「设置起始／结束于此的循环次数…」或轨道「⋯」的「设置本小节循环次数…」中填写共播放几遍（1–999，含第一遍），播放到该循环时播完这些遍数便自动向后继续；同一轨道的多个循环各自独立设定。有预设次数的循环在结束竖线左上方显示「×N」；留空或选「一直循环（清除次数）」则一直循环，直到按方块。次数随鼓谱保存。播放时按停止方块，会在本轮终点释放当前循环并继续后面的内容；也可用方块上方的「↻ 再循环…」输入再循环次数（1–999），先播完这些次数再继续。整谱「⋯」只提供「播放一次」和「循环整曲」；分段循环使用小节边界菜单设置。</dd>

42 |     <dt>Track loops and whole-score looping</dt><dd>Double-tap barlines, or hold them for 1 second, to set a track loop start and end. 【修改后 1.63】第 42 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
42 |     <dt>Track loops and whole-score looping</dt><dd>Double-tap barlines, or hold them for 0.5 seconds, to set a track loop start and end. The end must follow the start; loops on a track cannot overlap or pass the next loop. Existing loop ends can be changed. Each loop can preset how many times it plays in total (1–999, including the first pass) from Times for Loop Starting/Ending Here… in the barline menu or Loop Times for This Measure… in the track ⋯ menu; when playback reaches the loop it plays that many passes and then continues by itself. Several loops on one track keep their own counts. A loop with a preset count shows ×N above its closing barline; leave the count blank, or choose Loop until stopped, to loop until the square is pressed. Counts are saved with the score. During playback, the Stop square releases the active loop at the end of its current pass and continues into the following section; the ↻ Repeat… chip above it lets you type how many more times (1–999) the loop plays first. The score ⋯ menu offers Play Once and Loop Score; configure section loops in barline menus.</dd>

63 |     <dt>Beginning / Play / Stop</dt><dd>Select the first note of the current track and jump horizontally to its beginning without changing the vertical scroll position or starting playback. Play starts from the edit cursor and toggles pause or resume. During a track loop, the square button releases the loop and lets playback continue; otherwise it stops. While a loop plays, a ↻ Repeat… chip above the square opens a box: type how many more times the loop plays (1–999, or use −/+), choose Done, and playback continues once those passes finish; the chip shows the passes left. Keep looping in that box cancels a pending continue. The square alone still continues after the current pass. Space toggles play or pause.</dd>

63 |     <dt>Beginning / Play / Stop</dt><dd>Select the first note of the current track and jump horizontally to its beginning without changing the vertical scroll position or starting playback. Play starts from the edit cursor and toggles pause or resume. During a track loop, the square button releases the loop and lets playback continue; otherwise it stops. While a loop plays, a ↻ Repeat… chip above the square opens a box: type how many more times the loop plays (1–999, or use −/+), choose Done, and playback continues once those passes finish; the chip shows the passes left. Keep looping in that box cancels a pending continue. The square alone still continues after the current pass. A loop with a preset count shows its remaining passes as soon as it starts; the chip can change or cancel them for the current playback only. Space toggles play or pause.</dd>

==============================================================================
需求 2：所有长按激活改为 0.5 秒（1.62 → 1.63）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  所有长按激活改为 0.5 秒（原 1 秒）。
  · app.js：MENU_HOLD_MS 由 1000 改为 500。它同时用于五类菜单（符杆拍菜单、蓝点左右手菜单、小节线菜单、组拍号菜单、组速度弹窗）
    和 1.61 的音符键锁定，所以锁定音符也改为长按 0.5 秒（保持所有长按手感一致）。多选模式原有的 0.5 秒长按选整组、首页卡片
    0.5 秒长按菜单本来就是 0.5 秒，未改。
  · i18n.js 与中英文说明书中所有「长按 1 秒」改为「长按 0.5 秒」（meter.groupButton、bar.pickEnd、keys.lockHint 及说明书 7 处）；
    音频导出时长「1 秒至 30 分钟」与长按无关，未改。

本需求共 21 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

修改 19：source/ui/app.js
  安卓 assets/index.html：修改前 第 4293–4296 行；修改后 第 4323–4327 行
【修改前 1.62】第 141–144 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
141 | // 1.60: every control that opens a menu on a double-tap also opens it when held for one second
142 | // (finger, pen or left mouse button). The menu then keeps clear of the held point, and the click
143 | // that ends the hold is dropped, so lifting the finger never chooses a menu item.
144 | const MENU_HOLD_MS = 1000;
------------------------------------------------------------------------------
【修改后 1.63】第 141–145 行
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

143 | // dropped, so lifting the finger never chooses a menu item.
144 | // 1.63: the hold takes 0.5 s (was 1 s), and a menu opened by a double-tap keeps clear of the tap too.

25 |     <dt>Stems, sticking and beat menu</dt><dd>Tap a stem once to select it without moving the viewport. Tap the same stem twice within 0.25 seconds, or hold it for 1 second, to open its beat menu. Double-tap or hold the blue dot to set or clear L (left), R (right) or LR (both). Barline, group-meter and group-tempo menus also open with a double-tap or a 1-second hold. 25 |     <dt>Stems, sticking and beat menu</dt><dd>Tap a stem once to select it without moving the viewport. Tap the same stem twice within 0.25 seconds, or hold it for 0.5 seconds, to open its beat menu. Double-tap or hold the blue dot to set or clear L (left), R (right) or LR (both). Barline, group-meter and group-tempo menus also open with a double-tap or a 0.5-second hold. Whether opened by a double-tap or a hold, the menu appears clear of the tapped point, so lifting the finger or an extra tap never picks an item; choose menu items with one tap. The beat menu offers copy, paste, three/four subdivisions, split, group meter and sticking. Beat paste requires matching subdivision counts and compatible strokes. Multi-selection keeps its single-tap and long-press controls.</dd>

25 |     <dt>Stems, sticking and beat menu</dt><dd>Tap a stem once to select it without moving the viewport. Barline, group-meter and group-tempo menus also open with a double-tap or a 1-second hold. The beat menu offers copy, paste, three/four subdivisions, split, group meter and sticking. Beat paste requires matching subdivision counts and compatible strokes. Multi-selection keeps its single-tap and long-press controls.</dd>

Tap the same stem twice within 0.25 seconds, or hold it for 0.5 seconds, to open its beat menu. Double-tap or hold the blue dot to set or clear L (left), R (right) or LR (both). 26 |     <dt>Stroke keys</dt><dd>Djembe offers B (bass), T (tone), S (slap X), H (alternate high x), G (muted slap), P (light touch) and BT, BS, TT, TS, ST, SS, TB, HB grace combinations in playing order. Dununba, Sangba and Kenkeni use O (open), M (muted), K (stick on rim); Bell and Shekere use X. 42 |     <dt>Track loops and whole-score looping</dt><dd>Double-tap barlines, or hold them for 0.5 seconds, to set a track loop start and end. The end must follow the start; loops on a track cannot overlap or pass the next loop. Existing loop ends can be changed. Each loop can preset how many times it plays in total (1–999, including the first pass) from Times for Loop Starting/Ending Here… in the barline menu or Loop Times for This Measure… in the track ⋯ menu; when playback reaches the loop it plays that many passes and then continues by itself. Several loops on one track keep their own counts. A loop with a preset count shows ×N above its closing barline; leave the count blank, or choose Loop until stopped, to loop until the square is pressed. Counts are saved with the score. During playback, the Stop square releases the active loop at the end of its current pass and continues into the following section; the ↻ Repeat… chip above it lets you type how many more times (1–999) the loop plays first. The score ⋯ menu offers Play Once and Loop Score; configure section loops in barline menus.</dd>

==============================================================================
需求 3：双击打开的菜单也避开点击位置（1.62 → 1.63）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  双击打开的弹出菜单（以及速度弹窗）也避开点按位置，避免误点。
  · 原来只有长按会记录按压点（menuHoldPoint）。现在新增 setMenuAvoidPoint(node, x, y, id)：同时记录屏幕坐标和该点在控件上的
    相对位置（fx, fy）；新增 markMenuTap(event)：在完成双击的那次 click 中调用（键盘触发的 click 没有坐标，detail 为 0，跳过）；
    新增 menuAvoidPoint()：手指仍按着时用屏幕坐标，抬起后用控件当前位置换算，使避让点跟随谱面滚动。
  · 五处双击入口（小节线、符杆拍菜单、蓝点左右手菜单、组拍号、组速度）在打开菜单前调用 markMenuTap(event)。
  · clearOfMenuHold() 与 menuHoldRoom() 改为读取 menuAvoidPoint()。按压点在下一次按下（任何 pointerdown）时清除；
    长按松手时不再清除，而是转为跟随控件的相对位置。
  · 判断与摆放增加 2 px 容差（判断 42 px、摆放 44 px）：开发中发现鼠标事件坐标取整数，菜单被屏幕底边夹住时实际距离只有 39.25 px。
  · 速度弹窗的避让注释由「一秒长按」改为「长按或双击」。说明书相应句子改为「无论双击还是长按，弹出的菜单都会避开点按位置」。

本需求共 15 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

202 | // 1.63: remember where a menu was asked for, as a screen point and as a place on the control, so the
203 | // point follows the score if it scrolls after the finger has lifted.
204 | function setMenuAvoidPoint(node, x, y, id = null) {
205 |   const rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
206 |   menuHoldPoint = {id, x, y, node, fx: rect.width ? (x - rect.left) / rect.width : .5,
207 |     fy: rect.height ? (y - rect.top) / rect.height : .5};
208 | }
209 | 
210 | // 1.63: call with the click that completes a double-tap. Keyboard clicks carry no point (detail 0).
211 | function markMenuTap(event) {
212 |   if (event.detail && Number.isFinite(event.clientX)) setMenuAvoidPoint(event.currentTarget, event.clientX, event.clientY);
213 | }
214 | 
215 | function menuAvoidPoint() {
216 |   const point = menuHoldPoint;
217 |   if (!point || point.id !== null || !point.node.isConnected) return point;
218 |   const rect = point.node.getBoundingClientRect();
219 |   return {x: rect.left + point.fx * rect.width, y: rect.top + point.fy * rect.height};
220 | }
221 | 
222 | // Move a menu box {left, top, width, height} so it does not cover the point of the long press or
223 | // double-tap that opened it. Returns the same object when no move is needed.
224 | function clearOfMenuHold(box, bounds) {
225 |   const point = menuAvoidPoint();

25 |     <dt>Stems, sticking and beat menu</dt><dd>Tap a stem once to select it without moving the viewport. Tap the same stem twice within 0.25 seconds, or hold it for 1 second, to open its beat menu. Double-tap or hold the blue dot to set or clear L (left), R (right) or LR (both). Barline, group-meter and group-tempo menus also open with a double-tap or a 1-second hold. A menu opened by holding appears clear of your finger, so lifting it never picks an item; choose menu items with one tap. The beat menu offers copy, paste, three/four subdivisions, split, group meter and sticking. Beat paste requires matching subdivision counts and compatible strokes. Multi-selection keeps its single-tap and long-press controls.</dd>

【修改后 1.63】第 25 行

25 |     <dt>Stems, sticking and beat menu</dt><dd>Tap a stem once to select it without moving the viewport. Tap the same stem twice within 0.25 seconds, or hold it for 0.5 seconds, to open its beat menu. Double-tap or hold the blue dot to set or clear L (left), R (right) or LR (both). Barline, group-meter and group-tempo menus also open with a double-tap or a 0.5-second hold. Whether opened by a double-tap or a hold, the menu appears clear of the tapped point, so lifting the finger or an extra tap never picks an item; choose menu items with one tap. The beat menu offers copy, paste, three/four subdivisions, split, group meter and sticking. Beat paste requires matching subdivision counts and compatible strokes. Multi-selection keeps its single-tap and long-press controls.</dd>

■ 新增文件（修改前不存在）
  RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests/editor163.e2e.cjs  —— 1.63 功能的 Playwright 测试，全文见附录 C。
  tools/patch163.py。发布日期与 1.62 同为 2026-11-22，因此日期字符串未改。

■ 本轮开发中的注意点
  · 预设次数按「共几遍（含第一遍）」计，与方块上方「↻ 再循环…」的「再循环几次（本轮之后）」不同：预设 3 遍时，进入循环后按钮显示「再循环 2 次」。
  · 设置次数会停止正在进行的播放（与添加、移除、修改循环终点一致），因为它属于鼓谱修改；临时调整请用「↻ 再循环…」。
  · 测试 editor160/editor161 中的长按时长改为 0.65 秒（应打开）与 0.25 秒（不应打开）；editor163 另验证 0.3 秒不打开、0.7 秒打开。
  · 用 1.62 页面对照：双击小节线时菜单覆盖点按位置（距离 −89 px），1.63 为 44 px，说明 editor163 的避让检查确实有效。

==============================================================================
需求 1：多选模式中单个选中的符杆改为半透明蓝框，可看到竖线和音符（1.63 → 1.64）
==============================================================================

修改说明：
  多选模式中单个点选的符杆，蓝色选框改为半透明，透过选框能看到竖线和音符。
  · 原因：符杆按钮（.slot）叠在谱面图形（SVG）的上层，1.59 起单选样式 .multi-groups .slot.slot-picked 使用不透明底色 #dcecff，
    把下面的竖线、横梁和音头整个盖住；整组选中用的是拍组 .beat 的底色（在图形下层），不受影响。
  · style.css：.slot-picked 底色改为半透明蓝 #2876e829（约 16% 不透明度），边框改为半透明 #1d5b99b3（约 70%）。
  · 同一图层的多选粘贴覆盖预览 .slot.paste-preview 也有同样问题（盖住将被覆盖的原有音符），一并改为半透明
    （底色 #2876e824，上下边线 #2469bdb3）。这一项超出提示词字面范围，属同类问题。
  · 说明书（中英文）多选与粘贴两条补充说明。
  · 验证：新增测试 editor164.e2e.cjs 截图统计符杆区域内的深色像素（竖线、横梁、音头），选中前后基本相同（117→116）；
    用 1.63 页面对照时选中后为 0，说明测试确实能发现原问题。

本需求共 6 处代码修改。每处修改同时写入 Win11 源码与安卓页面 assets/index.html（两者代码相同，行号不同）。

■ 新增文件（修改前不存在）
  RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests/editor164.e2e.cjs  —— 1.64 的 Playwright 测试（含一个读取截图 PNG 的小解码器），全文见附录 D。
  tools/patch164.py。发布日期与 1.63 同为 2026-11-22，因此日期字符串未改。

==============================================================================
八、后续开发建议
==============================================================================
  · 新一轮修改沿用同样做法：先在 Win11 源码 source/ui 上设计修改，写成 tools/patchXXX.py 清单同时作用于安卓 index.html，
    构建后比对两平台页面差异，确认只剩原有安卓专用部分；然后重打包、签名、校验、跑测试。
  · 安卓专用代码只存在于 android/index.html（APK 内页面）。修改跟随、保存、下载相关代码时，两个平台写法不同，需分别处理。
  · 签名必须一直使用同一私钥，否则用户无法覆盖安装；versionCode 必须递增。
  · 建议在安卓真机和 Windows 11 Edge 上补做人工验收：长按 0.5 秒的手感、单击／双击／长按菜单的避让位置、预设循环次数与再循环计数、锁定输入。

==============================================================================
附录 A：测试 editor160.e2e.cjs（1.60 新增，1.63 更新后的全文）
==============================================================================
文件：RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests/editor160.e2e.cjs

  1 | // 1.60 editor checks in a real Chromium page (Playwright): Duplicate Track, splitting cells and
  2 | // changing subdivisions with notes in place, and the hold (0.5 s since 1.63) that opens every double-tap
  3 | // menu clear of the held point. Run once per built page:
  4 | //   node editor160.e2e.cjs ../../RhythmDesk.html ANDROID_ASSETS/index.html
  5 | const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
  6 | const path = require('node:path');
  7 | const {chromium} = require('playwright');
  8 | 
  9 | const CLEARANCE = 40;
 10 | async function rectOf(page, selector) {
 11 |   return page.$eval(selector, node => { const r = node.getBoundingClientRect(); return {left: r.left, top: r.top, right: r.right, bottom: r.bottom}; });
 12 | }
 13 | function assertClear(rect, point, label) {
 14 |   const covers = point.x > rect.left - CLEARANCE && point.x < rect.right + CLEARANCE &&
 15 |     point.y > rect.top - CLEARANCE && point.y < rect.bottom + CLEARANCE;
 16 |   assert(!covers, `${label} must keep ${CLEARANCE}px clear of the held point ${JSON.stringify(point)}; menu ${JSON.stringify(rect)}`);
 17 | }
 18 | async function centerOf(locator) {
 19 |   const box = await locator.boundingBox();
 20 |   return {x: box.x + box.width / 2, y: box.y + box.height / 2};
 21 | }
 22 | async function openDemo(page) {
 23 |   await page.evaluate(() => { openNewScore(demoProject(), ''); });
 24 |   await page.waitForSelector('.staff');
 25 | }
 26 | async function mouseHold(page, point, ms) {
 27 |   await page.mouse.move(point.x, point.y);
 28 |   await page.mouse.down();
 29 |   await page.waitForTimeout(ms);
 30 | }
 31 | const menuOpen = page => page.evaluate(() => !el.menu.hidden);
 32 | const beatMenuOpen = page => page.evaluate(() => !el.beatMenu.hidden);
 33 | 
 34 | async function duplicateChecks(page) {
 35 |   await openDemo(page);
 36 |   const before = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks.map(track => track.id));
 37 |   const djembe = 2; // "Djembe 1" in the demo
 38 |   await page.locator('.staff').nth(djembe).locator('.staff-head button.icon').click();
 39 |   const item = page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /复制本轨道|Duplicate Track/});
 40 |   assert.equal(await item.count(), 1, 'track menu offers Duplicate Track');
 41 |   await item.click();
 42 |   const result = await page.evaluate(index => {
 43 |     const source = doc.tracks[index], copy = doc.tracks[index + 1];
 44 |     const strip = track => { const {id, name, ...rest} = structuredClone(track); return rest; };
 45 |     return {count: doc.tracks.length, rows: document.querySelectorAll('.staff').length, source: source.name, name: copy.name,
 46 |       idUnique: new Set(doc.tracks.map(track => track.id)).size === doc.tracks.length,
 47 |       same: JSON.stringify(strip(source)) === JSON.stringify(strip(copy)), notes: copy.cells.filter(Boolean).length,
 48 |       cursor: cursor.track === copy.id, roundTrip: readProject(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(doc))).tracks.length};
 49 |   }, djembe);
 50 |   assert.equal(result.count, before.length + 1);
 51 |   assert.equal(result.rows, before.length + 1, 'the copy is drawn');
 52 |   assert(result.idUnique, 'copy has its own id');
 53 |   assert(result.same, 'copy keeps notes, sticking, measures, loops, samples and mix');
 54 |   assert(result.notes > 0, 'notes are copied');
 55 |   assert.match(result.name, /^Djembe 1 (副本|copy)$/);
 56 |   assert(result.cursor, 'cursor moves to the copy');
 57 |   assert.equal(result.roundTrip, before.length + 1, 'saved score with the copy reopens');
 58 |   // A paired drum: the copy goes below the Bell/Drum pair and the pair stays together.
 59 |   const pair = await page.evaluate(() => {
 60 |     const drum = doc.tracks.find(track => track.type === 'kenkeni');
 61 |     duplicateTrackWithContent(drum);
 62 |     const blocks = trackBlocks(doc).map(block => block.map(track => track.name).join('+'));
 63 |     return {blocks, copy: doc.tracks[doc.tracks.findIndex(track => track === drum) + 1].name};
 64 |   });
 65 |   assert(pair.blocks.includes('Kenkeni Bell+Kenkeni'), 'original Bell/Drum pair intact: ' + pair.blocks);
 66 |   assert.match(pair.copy, /^Kenkeni (副本|copy)$/);
 67 |   // Duplicating twice numbers the names; undo removes the copy in one step.
 68 |   const names = await page.evaluate(() => {
 69 |     const drum = doc.tracks.find(track => track.name === 'Kenkeni');
 70 |     duplicateTrackWithContent(drum);
 71 |     const name = doc.tracks.filter(track => track.name.startsWith('Kenkeni ')).map(track => track.name);
 72 |     el.undo.click();
 73 |     return {name, after: doc.tracks.filter(track => track.name.startsWith('Kenkeni ')).length};
 74 |   });
 75 |   assert.deepEqual(names.name.sort(), ['Kenkeni Bell', 'Kenkeni 副本', 'Kenkeni 副本 2'].sort());
 76 |   assert.equal(names.after, 2, 'undo removes one copy');
 77 |   console.log('  PASS Duplicate Track: menu item, full content, ids, placement below pairs, naming, undo, reopen.');
 78 | }
 79 | 
 80 | async function splitChecks(page) {
 81 |   await openDemo(page);
 82 |   // Djembe 1, first beat holds "S.T": notes on cells 0 and 2 of a 3-cell group.
 83 |   const track = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks[2].id);
 84 |   const slots = page.locator(`.staff[data-track-id="${track}"] .slot`);
 85 |   await slots.nth(0).click();
 86 |   await page.waitForTimeout(300);
 87 |   // ♫ split key while notes are written: no "clear notes first" warning, note kept.
 88 |   await page.locator('.split-key').click();
 89 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /^2 /}).click();
 90 |   let state = await page.evaluate(id => {
 91 |     const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id);
 92 |     return {warning: el.noteChangeWarning.open, beat: trackGrid(doc, track).beats[0], cells: track.cells.slice(0, 4)};
 93 |   }, track);
 94 |   assert.equal(state.warning, false, 'no clear-notes warning');
 95 |   assert.deepEqual(state.beat, [2, 1, 1]);
 96 |   assert.deepEqual(state.cells, ['S', null, null, 'T'], 'notes stay on their cells after the split');
 97 |   // Digit key 4 on the same cell: 2 -> 4 parts keeps the note.
 98 |   await page.keyboard.press('4');
 99 |   state = await page.evaluate(id => {
100 |     const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id);
101 |     return {beat: trackGrid(doc, track).beats[0], cells: track.cells.slice(0, 6)};
102 |   }, track);
103 |   assert.deepEqual(state.beat, [4, 1, 1]);
104 |   assert.deepEqual(state.cells, ['S', null, null, null, null, 'T']);
105 |   // Write a note on the second sub-cell, then merge back to 1 part: the overlap asks to merge.
106 |   await page.evaluate(id => { selectSlot(id, 0, 2); writeStroke('B', 0); }, track);
107 |   let asked = '';
108 |   page.once('dialog', dialog => { asked = dialog.message(); dialog.accept(); });
109 |   await page.keyboard.press('1');
110 |   state = await page.evaluate(id => {
111 |     const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id);
112 |     return {beat: trackGrid(doc, track).beats[0], cells: track.cells.slice(0, 3)};
113 |   }, track);
114 |   assert.match(asked, /合并|merged/, 'merge confirmation');
115 |   assert.equal(state.beat, 3);
116 |   assert.deepEqual(state.cells, ['S', null, 'T'], 'merge keeps the first note of the cell');
117 |   // Beat menu 3/4 switch on a beat with notes (double-tap the stem, then the menu item).
118 |   await page.evaluate(id => selectSlot(id, 1, 0), track);
119 |   await slots.nth(3).click(); await slots.nth(3).click();
120 |   assert(await beatMenuOpen(page), 'double-tap still opens the beat menu');
121 |   await page.locator('#beatMenu button', {hasText: /3\/4|Tuplet/}).click();
122 |   state = await page.evaluate(id => {
123 |     const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id);
124 |     const grid = trackGrid(doc, track);
125 |     return {warning: el.noteChangeWarning.open, sizes: grid.beats.slice(0, 2), notes: track.cells.filter(Boolean).length};
126 |   }, track);
127 |   assert.equal(state.warning, false);
128 |   assert.deepEqual(state.sizes, [3, 4]);
129 |   // Track menu: split the whole track into 2 parts with notes written.
130 |   const notesBefore = await page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id).cells.filter(Boolean).length, track);
131 |   await page.locator('.staff').nth(2).locator('.staff-head button.icon').click();
132 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /统一拆分本轨道|Split track/}).click();
133 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /^2 /}).click();
134 |   state = await page.evaluate(id => {
135 |     const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id);
136 |     return {warning: el.noteChangeWarning.open, first: trackGrid(doc, track).beats[0], notes: track.cells.filter(Boolean).length};
137 |   }, track);
138 |   assert.equal(state.warning, false, 'bulk split does not ask to clear notes');
139 |   assert.deepEqual(state.first, [2, 2, 2]);
140 |   assert.equal(state.notes, notesBefore, 'bulk split keeps every note');
141 |   // A score without per-track measures: the beat-menu 3/4 switch changes that beat on every track.
142 |   await openDemo(page);
143 |   const shared = await page.evaluate(() => {
144 |     const notes = () => doc.tracks.reduce((count, track) => count + track.cells.filter(Boolean).length, 0);
145 |     const before = notes();
146 |     selectSlot(doc.tracks[2].id, 0, 0);
147 |     changeTuplet();
148 |     return {warning: el.noteChangeWarning.open, before, after: notes(), beat: doc.beats[0], measures: doc.tracks.some(track => track.measures)};
149 |   });
150 |   assert.deepEqual(shared, {warning: false, before: shared.before, after: shared.before, beat: 4, measures: false});
151 |   // Score ⋯ "all groups to 4" keeps every note.
152 |   const all = await page.evaluate(() => {
153 |     const notes = () => doc.tracks.reduce((count, track) => count + track.cells.filter(Boolean).length, 0);
154 |     const before = notes();
155 |     applyBeatSizes(doc.beats.map(() => 4), 4);
156 |     return {warning: el.noteChangeWarning.open, before, after: notes(), beats: [...new Set(doc.beats)]};
157 |   });
158 |   assert.deepEqual(all, {warning: false, before: all.before, after: all.before, beats: [4]});
159 |   console.log('  PASS split with notes: ♫ key, digit keys, merge confirmation, beat-menu 3/4 switch, bulk track split, shared-grid 3/4 switch, all groups to 4.');
160 | }
161 | 
162 | async function holdChecks(page, touch) {
163 |   await openDemo(page);
164 |   const track = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks[2].id);
165 |   const row = page.locator(`.staff[data-track-id="${track}"]`);
166 |   const hold = touch ? touchHold : (point, ms) => mouseHold(page, point, ms);
167 |   const release = touch ? touchEnd : () => page.mouse.up();
168 |   async function touchHold(point, ms) {
169 |     await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [{x: point.x, y: point.y}]});
170 |     await page.waitForTimeout(ms);
171 |   }
172 |   async function touchEnd() { await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchEnd', touchPoints: []}); }
173 |   const cdp = touch ? await page.context().newCDPSession(page) : null;
174 | 
175 |   // Short press (0.5 s) opens nothing.
176 |   const slot = row.locator('.slot').nth(4);
177 |   let point = await centerOf(slot);
178 |   await hold(point, 250); await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
179 |   assert.equal(await beatMenuOpen(page), false, 'a 0.25 s press does not open the beat menu');
180 |   // A 0.5 s hold (1 s before 1.63) on a stem opens the beat menu clear of the finger; releasing chooses nothing.
181 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
182 |   point = await centerOf(slot);
183 |   await hold(point, 650);
184 |   assert(await beatMenuOpen(page), 'held stem opens the beat menu');
185 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, '#beatMenu'), point, 'beat menu');
186 |   const selected = await page.evaluate(() => ({beat: cursor.beat, slot: cursor.slot, undo: undoStack.length}));
187 |   await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
188 |   assert(await beatMenuOpen(page), 'releasing the hold keeps the beat menu open');
189 |   assert.deepEqual({beat: selected.beat, slot: selected.slot}, {beat: 1, slot: 1}, 'held stem is selected');
190 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => undoStack.length), selected.undo, 'release does not run a menu item');
191 |   // Movement during the hold cancels it.
192 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
193 |   point = await centerOf(row.locator('.slot').nth(6));
194 |   if (touch) {
195 |     await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [{x: point.x, y: point.y}]});
196 |     await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchMove', touchPoints: [{x: point.x + 25, y: point.y}]});
197 |   } else { await page.mouse.move(point.x, point.y); await page.mouse.down(); await page.mouse.move(point.x + 25, point.y, {steps: 3}); }
198 |   await page.waitForTimeout(1100); await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(300);
199 |   assert.equal(await beatMenuOpen(page), false, 'moving during the hold cancels it');
200 |   // Blue sticking dot -> sticking menu.
201 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
202 |   point = await centerOf(row.locator('.slot').nth(5).locator('.hand-dot'));
203 |   await hold(point, 650);
204 |   assert(await menuOpen(page), 'held sticking dot opens the sticking menu');
205 |   assert(await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /左手|Left/}).count() > 0, 'it is the sticking menu');
206 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, '#menu'), point, 'sticking menu');
207 |   await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
208 |   assert(await menuOpen(page), 'sticking menu stays open after release');
209 |   // Barline -> measure menu.
210 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
211 |   point = await centerOf(row.locator('.barline').nth(1));
212 |   await hold(point, 650);
213 |   assert(await menuOpen(page), 'held barline opens the barline menu');
214 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, '#menu'), point, 'barline menu');
215 |   const items = await page.locator('#menu button').count();
216 |   await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
217 |   assert(await menuOpen(page) && await page.locator('#menu button').count() === items, 'barline menu unchanged after release');
218 |   // Group meter -> meter menu.
219 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
220 |   const meter = row.locator('.group-meter').nth(2);
221 |   await meter.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
222 |   point = await centerOf(meter);
223 |   await hold(point, 650);
224 |   assert(await menuOpen(page), 'held group meter opens the meter menu');
225 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, '#menu'), point, 'group meter menu');
226 |   await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
227 |   // Group tempo -> tempo dialog away from the finger.
228 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
229 |   point = await centerOf(row.locator('.group-tempo').nth(2));
230 |   await hold(point, 650);
231 |   assert(await page.locator('dialog.tempo-editor[open]').count() === 1, 'held group tempo opens the tempo editor');
232 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, 'dialog.tempo-editor[open]'), point, 'tempo editor');
233 |   await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
234 |   assert(await page.locator('dialog.tempo-editor[open]').count() === 1, 'tempo editor stays open after release');
235 |   await page.locator('dialog.tempo-editor .tempo-cancel').click();
236 |   // Multi-selection keeps its 0.5 s whole-group hold and opens no beat menu.
237 |   await page.evaluate(id => startMultiGroups(doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id)), track);
238 |   point = await centerOf(row.locator('.slot').nth(4));
239 |   await hold(point, 650); await release(); await page.waitForTimeout(450);
240 |   assert.equal(await beatMenuOpen(page), false, 'multi-selection hold opens no beat menu');
241 |   assert(await page.evaluate(() => multiGroups?.picks.length > 0), 'multi-selection hold still picks the group');
242 |   await page.evaluate(() => finishMultiGroups());
243 |   console.log(`  PASS ${touch ? 'touch' : 'mouse'} 0.5 s hold: stem, sticking dot, barline, group meter, group tempo; menus clear of the point; release, short press, movement and multi-selection.`);
244 | }
245 | 
246 | (async () => {
247 |   const pages = process.argv.slice(2);
248 |   if (!pages.length) throw Error('Usage: node editor160.e2e.cjs PAGE.html [PAGE.html ...]');
249 |   const browser = await chromium.launch();
250 |   try {
251 |     for (const file of pages) {
252 |       console.log('Page ' + path.resolve(file));

254 |       for (const [label, options] of [['desktop', {viewport: {width: 1280, height: 800}}],
255 |         ['phone', {viewport: {width: 390, height: 780}, hasTouch: true, isMobile: true, deviceScaleFactor: 1}]]) {

附录 B：测试 editor161.e2e.cjs（1.61 新增，1.62／1.63 更新后的全文）
