  1 | // 1.61 editor checks in a real Chromium page (Playwright), updated for 1.62: the loop-repeat chip (type
  2 | // how many more passes, then continue; or keep looping), the tall blue box over the playing note of
  3 | // every track, and the note-key lock for tap entry. Run once per built page:
  4 | //   node editor161.e2e.cjs ../../RhythmDesk.html ANDROID_ASSETS/index.html
  5 | const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
  6 | const path = require('node:path');
  7 | const {chromium} = require('playwright');
  8 | 
  9 | async function openDemo(page, loops = false) {
 10 |   await page.evaluate(loops => {
 11 |     const project = demoProject();
 12 |     project.bpm = 200;
 13 |     if (loops) for (const track of project.tracks) track.loops = [{from: 0, to: 1}];
 14 |     openNewScore(project, '');
 15 |   }, loops);
 16 |   await page.waitForSelector('.staff');
 17 | }
 18 | // Record the playing step of one track every animation frame until `until` returns true.
 19 | async function watchSteps(page, trackIndex, ms) {
 20 |   return page.evaluate(([index, ms]) => new Promise(resolve => {
 21 |     const id = doc.tracks[index].id, steps = [];
 22 |     const started = performance.now();
 23 |     const tick = () => {
 24 |       const step = playingPositions.get(id);
 25 |       if (steps.at(-1) !== step) steps.push(step);
 26 |       if (performance.now() - started < ms && engine.running) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 27 |       else resolve(steps);
 28 |     };
 29 |     tick();
 30 |   }), [trackIndex, ms]);
 31 | }
 32 | const earlyInPass = page => page.waitForFunction(() => {
 33 |   const step = playingPositions.get(doc.tracks[2].id);
 34 |   return step >= 1 && step <= 3;
 35 | }, null, {timeout: 5000, polling: 'raf'});
 36 | const returns = steps => steps.filter((step, i) => i && step >= 0 && step < steps[i - 1]).length;
 37 | 
 38 | async function repeatChecks(page) {
 39 |   await openDemo(page, true);
 40 |   await page.click('#play');
 41 |   await page.waitForFunction(() => !el.loopRepeat.hidden, null, {timeout: 5000});
 42 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#loopRepeat'), /再循环…|Repeat…/, 'chip appears during the loop');
 43 |   const chip = await page.locator('#loopRepeat').boundingBox(), stop = await page.locator('#stop').boundingBox();
 44 |   assert(Math.abs(chip.x + chip.width / 2 - (stop.x + stop.width / 2)) < 2 && chip.y + chip.height <= stop.y,
 45 |     'chip sits above the square');
 46 |   // Wait for a loop return, then choose "2 more" early in a pass: a choice in the last ~0.12 s before
 47 |   // the loop end applies from the next pass, exactly like the square button.
 48 |   let steps = await watchSteps(page, 2, 1500);
 49 |   assert(returns(steps) >= 1, 'the section loops: ' + steps);
 50 |   await earlyInPass(page);
 51 |   await page.click('#loopRepeat');
 52 |   const box = page.locator('dialog.loop-repeat-editor[open]');
 53 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 1, 'the chip opens the repeat-count box');
 54 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), '1');
 55 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('.loop-keep').count(), 0, 'keep looping appears only once a continue is pending');
 56 |   await box.locator('input').fill('2');
 57 |   await box.locator('.tempo-confirm').click();
 58 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 0);
 59 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#loopRepeat'), /再循环 2 次|2 more/);
 60 |   assert.match(await page.getAttribute('#stop', 'title'), /再循环 2 次|2 more/);
 61 |   // The chip and the Stop tooltip count down at each return to the loop start.
 62 |   await page.waitForFunction(() => /再循环 1 次|1 more/.test(el.loopRepeat.textContent) && /1/.test(el.stop.title), null, {timeout: 3000});
 63 |   // Exactly two more returns to the loop start, then playback continues into measure 2 (step 12+).
 64 |   steps = await watchSteps(page, 2, 6000);
 65 |   const pastLoop = steps.findIndex(step => step >= 12);
 66 |   assert(pastLoop > 0, 'playback continued past the loop: ' + steps);
 67 |   assert.equal(returns(steps.slice(0, pastLoop)), 2, 'two more passes before continuing: ' + steps);
 68 |   assert(await page.evaluate(() => el.loopRepeat.hidden || !engine.running), 'chip hides after the loop');
 69 |   await page.evaluate(() => stopPlayback());
 70 | 
 71 |   // Any count: values outside 1–999, fractions and blanks are refused; Cancel changes nothing;
 72 |   // 37 is accepted with Enter; −/+ adjust; Keep looping cancels the pending continue.
 73 |   await page.click('#play');
 74 |   await page.waitForFunction(() => !el.loopRepeat.hidden, null, {timeout: 5000});
 75 |   await page.click('#loopRepeat');
 76 |   await box.locator('input').fill('5');
 77 |   await box.locator('.tempo-cancel').click();
 78 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 0);
 79 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#loopRepeat'), /再循环…|Repeat…/, 'Cancel changes nothing');
 80 |   assert(await page.evaluate(() => playingLoops.every(gate => !gate.released)));
 81 |   await page.click('#loopRepeat');
 82 |   for (const bad of ['0', '1000', '2.5', '']) {
 83 |     await box.locator('input').fill(bad);
 84 |     await box.locator('.tempo-confirm').click();
 85 |     assert.equal(await box.count(), 1, 'refuses ' + JSON.stringify(bad));
 86 |   }
 87 |   await box.locator('input').fill('37');
 88 |   await box.locator('input').press('Enter');
 89 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 0, 'Enter confirms');
 90 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#loopRepeat'), /再循环 3[67] 次|3[67] more/);
 91 |   await page.click('#loopRepeat');
 92 |   assert.match(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), /^3[67]$/, 'the box shows the passes left');
 93 |   const shown = Number(await box.locator('input').inputValue());
 94 |   await box.locator('.tempo-controls button').nth(1).click();
 95 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), String(shown + 1), '+ adds one');
 96 |   await box.locator('.tempo-controls button').nth(0).click();
 97 |   await box.locator('.tempo-controls button').nth(0).click();
 98 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), String(shown - 1), '− removes one');
 99 |   await box.locator('.loop-keep').click();
100 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 0);
101 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#loopRepeat'), /再循环…|Repeat…/);
102 |   steps = await watchSteps(page, 2, 3500);
103 |   assert(returns(steps) >= 2 && steps.every(step => step < 12), 'keeps looping after cancel: ' + steps);
104 |   await earlyInPass(page);
105 |   await page.click('#stop');
106 |   steps = await watchSteps(page, 2, 3000);
107 |   const after = steps.findIndex(step => step >= 12);
108 |   assert(after > 0 && returns(steps.slice(0, after)) <= 1, 'square continues after the current pass: ' + steps);
109 |   await page.evaluate(() => stopPlayback());
110 |   console.log('  PASS loop repeat: chip above square, typed count (2 → exactly 2 passes, chip counts down; 37 accepted; 0/1000/2.5/blank refused), −/+, Cancel, keep looping, square unchanged.');
111 | }

113 | async function playingBoxChecks(page, shot) {
114 |   await openDemo(page);
115 |   await page.click('#play');
116 |   await page.waitForFunction(() => document.querySelectorAll('.slot.playing').length > 0, null, {timeout: 5000});
117 |   await page.waitForTimeout(400);
118 |   const state = await page.evaluate(() => {
119 |     const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('.staff')];
120 |     const slot = document.querySelector('.slot.playing');
121 |     const box = getComputedStyle(slot, '::after');
122 |     return {rows: rows.length, marked: rows.map(row => row.querySelectorAll('.slot.playing').length),
123 |       border: box.borderTopColor, width: parseFloat(box.width), height: parseFloat(box.height),
124 |       top: parseFloat(box.top), slot: slot.offsetHeight, style: box.borderTopStyle};
125 |   });
126 |   assert(state.marked.every(count => count === 1), 'every track marks exactly one playing note: ' + state.marked);
127 |   assert.equal(state.style, 'solid');
128 |   // Tall rectangle: from the beam (stem top at 15/61 of the beat) to below the note head (bass head ends near 56/61).
129 |   assert(state.height > 2 * state.width, 'the box is a tall rectangle: ' + JSON.stringify(state));
130 |   assert(state.top <= state.slot * 15 / 61 && state.top + state.height >= state.slot * 56 / 61, 'the box covers the stem and note head: ' + JSON.stringify(state));
131 |   assert.match(state.border, /rgb\(40, 118, 232\)|rgb\(\d+, \d+, \d+\)/);
132 |   if (shot) await page.screenshot({path: shot});
133 |   await page.click('#play'); // pause keeps the boxes
134 |   await page.waitForTimeout(400);
135 |   assert(await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.slot.playing').length === doc.tracks.length), 'pause keeps boxes');
136 |   await page.evaluate(() => stopPlayback());
137 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => document.querySelectorAll('.slot.playing').length), 0, 'stop removes boxes');
138 |   console.log(`  PASS playing box: ${state.rows} tracks each show one ${state.width}×${state.height.toFixed(1)} px tall blue box over stem and note head; pause keeps, stop clears.`);
139 | }

141 | async function lockChecks(page, touch) {
142 |   await openDemo(page);
143 |   const cdp = touch ? await page.context().newCDPSession(page) : null;
144 |   const hold = async (locator, ms = 650) => {
145 |     const box = await locator.boundingBox();
146 |     const x = box.x + box.width / 2, y = box.y + box.height / 2;
147 |     if (touch) {
148 |       await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [{x, y}]});
149 |       await page.waitForTimeout(ms);
150 |       await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchEnd', touchPoints: []});
151 |     } else { await page.mouse.move(x, y); await page.mouse.down(); await page.waitForTimeout(ms); await page.mouse.up(); }
152 |     await page.waitForTimeout(450);
153 |   };
154 |   const tap = async locator => {
155 |     if (touch) await locator.tap(); else await locator.click();
156 |     await page.waitForTimeout(300);
157 |   };
158 |   const djembe = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks[2].id);
159 |   const slots = page.locator(`.staff[data-track-id="${djembe}"] .slot`);
160 |   await tap(slots.nth(0));
161 |   const cellsOf = () => page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).cells.slice(0, 12), djembe);
162 |   const before = await cellsOf();
163 |   const undo = await page.evaluate(() => undoStack.length);
164 |   // Hold B for 1 s: locked, nothing written at the cursor.
165 |   await hold(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="B"]'));
166 |   assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => noteLock && {stroke: noteLock.stroke}), {stroke: 'B'});
167 |   assert(await page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="B"]').evaluate(node => node.classList.contains('locked')));
168 |   assert.deepEqual(await cellsOf(), before, 'locking writes nothing');
169 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => undoStack.length), undo);
170 |   // Tap empty stems: B is written there and the cursor stays.
171 |   await tap(slots.nth(1));
172 |   await tap(slots.nth(4));
173 |   let cells = await cellsOf();
174 |   assert.equal(cells[1], 'B'); assert.equal(cells[4], 'B');
175 |   assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => [cursor.beat, cursor.slot]), [1, 1], 'cursor stays on the tapped stem');
176 |   // Tap the same stem again: cleared. A quick double tap writes and clears without opening the beat menu.
177 |   await tap(slots.nth(4));
178 |   assert.equal((await cellsOf())[4], null);
179 |   if (!touch) { await slots.nth(5).click(); await slots.nth(5).click(); await page.waitForTimeout(300); }
180 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => el.beatMenu.hidden), true, 'double tap does not open the beat menu while locked');
181 |   // A 0.5 s hold on a stem still opens the beat menu and writes nothing.
182 |   cells = await cellsOf();
183 |   await hold(slots.nth(7));
184 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => el.beatMenu.hidden), false, 'hold still opens the beat menu');
185 |   assert.deepEqual(await cellsOf(), cells);
186 |   await page.evaluate(() => closePopups());
187 |   // Another instrument without B: only selects.
188 |   const kenkeni = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks.find(track => track.type === 'kenkeni').id);
189 |   const kenkeniCells = await page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).cells.join(), kenkeni);
190 |   await tap(page.locator(`.staff[data-track-id="${kenkeni}"] .slot`).nth(1));
191 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).cells.join(), kenkeni), kenkeniCells);
192 |   assert.match(await page.textContent('#status'), /没有锁定的音符|has no/);
193 |   // Back on Djembe the rebuilt keyboard still shows the lock.
194 |   await tap(slots.nth(2));
195 |   assert.equal((await cellsOf())[2], 'B');
196 |   assert(await page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="B"]').evaluate(node => node.classList.contains('locked')));
197 |   // Hold Rest: the lock moves; tapping clears notes.
198 |   await hold(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke=""]'));
199 |   assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => noteLock && {stroke: noteLock.stroke}), {stroke: null});
200 |   await tap(slots.nth(2));
201 |   assert.equal((await cellsOf())[2], null);
202 |   // Tap the locked key: unlock, nothing written.
203 |   cells = await cellsOf();
204 |   await tap(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke=""]'));
205 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => noteLock), undefined);
206 |   assert.deepEqual(await cellsOf(), cells, 'unlocking tap writes nothing');
207 |   // Normal taps work again (stem selects; key writes and advances).
208 |   await tap(slots.nth(9));
209 |   await tap(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="T"]'));
210 |   assert.equal((await cellsOf())[9], 'T');
211 |   assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(() => [cursor.beat, cursor.slot]), [3, 1], 'normal entry advances');
212 |   if (!touch) {
213 |     // Esc and closing the keyboard both unlock.
214 |     await hold(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="S"]'));
215 |     await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
216 |     assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => noteLock), undefined, 'Esc unlocks');
217 |     await hold(page.locator('#keys .key[data-stroke="S"]'));
218 |     await page.click('#showKeys');
219 |     assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => noteLock), undefined, 'closing the keyboard unlocks');
220 |   }
221 |   console.log(`  PASS ${touch ? 'touch' : 'mouse'} note lock: hold to lock, tap to enter/clear, cursor stays, beat menu by hold, other instruments, Rest lock, unlock.`);
222 | }
223 | 
224 | (async () => {
225 |   const pages = process.argv.slice(2);
226 |   if (!pages.length) throw Error('Usage: node editor161.e2e.cjs PAGE.html [PAGE.html ...]');
227 |   const browser = await chromium.launch({args: ['--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required']});
228 |   try {
229 |     for (const file of pages) {
230 |       console.log('Page ' + path.resolve(file));
231 |       const url = 'file://' + path.resolve(file);
232 |       for (const [label, options] of [['desktop', {viewport: {width: 1280, height: 800}}],
233 |         ['phone', {viewport: {width: 390, height: 780}, hasTouch: true, isMobile: true, deviceScaleFactor: 1}]]) {
234 |         const context = await browser.newContext({...options, locale: 'zh-CN'});
235 |         const page = await context.newPage();
236 |         const errors = [];
237 |         page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
238 |         await page.goto(url);
239 |         await page.waitForSelector('#rhythmGrid');
240 |         if (label === 'desktop') { await repeatChecks(page); await playingBoxChecks(page, process.env.SHOT); await lockChecks(page, false); }
241 |         else { await playingBoxChecks(page, process.env.SHOT_PHONE); await lockChecks(page, true); }
242 |         assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'no page errors on ' + label);
243 |         await context.close();
244 |       }
245 |     }
246 |   } finally { await browser.close(); }
247 |   console.log('PASS 1.61 editor checks on ' + pages.length + ' page(s).');
248 | })().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

附录 C：测试 editor163.e2e.cjs（1.63 新增）

  1 | // 1.63 editor checks in a real Chromium page (Playwright): a preset count on each loop region (two
  2 | // loops on one track play their own number of passes and then continue by themselves), the 0.5 s
  3 | // hold, and menus opened by a double-tap keeping clear of the tapped point. Run once per built page:
  4 | //   node editor163.e2e.cjs ../../RhythmDesk.html ANDROID_ASSETS/index.html
  5 | const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
  6 | const path = require('node:path');
  7 | const {chromium} = require('playwright');
  8 | 
  9 | const CLEARANCE = 40;
 10 | function assertClear(rect, point, label) {
 11 |   const covers = point.x > rect.left - CLEARANCE && point.x < rect.right + CLEARANCE &&
 12 |     point.y > rect.top - CLEARANCE && point.y < rect.bottom + CLEARANCE;
 13 |   assert(!covers, `${label} must keep ${CLEARANCE}px clear of the tapped point ${JSON.stringify(point)}; menu ${JSON.stringify(rect)}`);
 14 | }
 15 | const rectOf = (page, selector) => page.$eval(selector, node => {
 16 |   const r = node.getBoundingClientRect(); return {left: r.left, top: r.top, right: r.right, bottom: r.bottom};
 17 | });
 18 | async function centerOf(locator) {
 19 |   const box = await locator.boundingBox();
 20 |   return {x: box.x + box.width / 2, y: box.y + box.height / 2};
 21 | }
 22 | async function openDemo(page, setup = '') {
 23 |   await page.evaluate(setup => {
 24 |     const project = demoProject();
 25 |     project.bpm = 300;
 26 |     new Function('project', setup)(project);
 27 |     openNewScore(project, '');
 28 |   }, setup);
 29 |   await page.waitForSelector('.staff');
 30 | }
 31 | const menuItems = page => page.locator('#menu button').allTextContents();
 32 | 
 33 | async function presetChecks(page) {
 34 |   // Djembe 1 (track 3) gets two loops: measure 1 and measure 2.
 35 |   await openDemo(page, 'project.tracks[2].loops = [{from: 0, to: 1}, {from: 1, to: 2}];');
 36 |   const djembe = await page.evaluate(() => doc.tracks[2].id);
 37 |   const row = page.locator(`.staff[data-track-id="${djembe}"]`);
 38 |   const shared = row.locator('.barline').nth(1); // ends loop 1 and starts loop 2
 39 |   await shared.dblclick();
 40 |   let items = await menuItems(page);
 41 |   assert(items.some(item => /设置结束于此的循环次数…（一直循环）|Times for Loop Ending Here… \(until stopped\)/.test(item)), items.join(' | '));
 42 |   assert(items.some(item => /设置起始于此的循环次数…（一直循环）/.test(item)));
 43 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /结束于此的循环次数|Loop Ending Here/}).click();
 44 |   const box = page.locator('dialog.loop-times-editor[open]');
 45 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 1);
 46 |   assert.match(await box.locator('h3').textContent(), /Djembe 1.*第 1 小节/);
 47 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), '', 'no count yet: blank means loop until stopped');
 48 |   for (const bad of ['0', '1000', '1.5']) {
 49 |     await box.locator('input').fill(bad);
 50 |     await box.locator('.tempo-confirm').click();
 51 |     assert.equal(await box.count(), 1, 'refuses ' + bad);
 52 |   }
 53 |   await box.locator('input').fill('2');
 54 |   await box.locator('input').press('Enter');
 55 |   assert.equal(await box.count(), 0);
 56 |   await shared.dblclick();
 57 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /起始于此的循环次数/}).click();
 58 |   await box.locator('.tempo-controls button').nth(1).click(); // blank + 1 -> 2
 59 |   await box.locator('.tempo-controls button').nth(1).click(); // 3
 60 |   await box.locator('.tempo-confirm').click();
 61 |   let state = await page.evaluate(id => ({loops: doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).loops,
 62 |     marks: [...document.querySelectorAll(`.staff[data-track-id="${id}"] .loop-times`)].map(node => node.textContent),
 63 |     reopened: readProject(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(doc))).tracks.find(track => track.id === id).loops}), djembe);
 64 |   assert.deepEqual(state.loops, [{from: 0, to: 1, times: 2}, {from: 1, to: 2, times: 3}]);
 65 |   assert.deepEqual(state.marks, ['×2', '×3'], 'closing barlines show the counts');
 66 |   assert.deepEqual(state.reopened, state.loops, 'counts are saved with the score');
 67 |   // Track menu: the loop of the selected measure.
 68 |   await row.locator('.slot').nth(1).click();
 69 |   await row.locator('.staff-head button.icon').click();
 70 |   items = await menuItems(page);
 71 |   assert(items.some(item => /设置本小节循环次数…（共 2 遍）|Loop Times for This Measure… \(2×\)/.test(item)), items.join(' | '));
 72 |   await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
 73 | 
 74 |   // Playback: 2 passes of measure 1, 3 passes of measure 2, then the score ends by itself.
 75 |   const run = await page.evaluate(id => new Promise(resolve => {
 76 |     const steps = [], chips = new Set();
 77 |     el.play.click();
 78 |     const started = performance.now();
 79 |     const tick = () => {
 80 |       const step = playingPositions.get(id);
 81 |       if (step !== undefined && steps.at(-1) !== step) steps.push(step);
 82 |       if (!el.loopRepeat.hidden) chips.add(el.loopRepeat.textContent);
 83 |       if (performance.now() - started > 12000) resolve({steps, chips: [...chips], timeout: true});
 84 |       else if (!pending && !engine.running && steps.length > 5) resolve({steps, chips: [...chips], timeout: false});
 85 |       else requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 86 |     };
 87 |     requestAnimationFrame(tick);
 88 |   }), djembe);
 89 |   const returns = (from, to) => run.steps.filter((step, i) => i && step < run.steps[i - 1] && step >= from && step < to).length;
 90 |   assert.equal(run.timeout, false, 'playback finished without pressing anything');
 91 |   assert.equal(returns(0, 12), 1, 'measure 1 played twice: ' + run.steps);
 92 |   assert.equal(returns(12, 24), 2, 'measure 2 played three times: ' + run.steps);
 93 |   assert.equal(Math.max(...run.steps), 23);
 94 |   assert(run.chips.some(text => /再循环 1 次|1 more/.test(text)) && run.chips.some(text => /再循环 2 次|2 more/.test(text)),
 95 |     'the chip shows the preset passes left: ' + run.chips);
 96 | 
 97 |   // The chip still overrides a preset for this playback only: keep looping, then the square.
 98 |   await page.click('#play');
 99 |   await page.waitForFunction(() => !el.loopRepeat.hidden && /再循环 1 次|1 more/.test(el.loopRepeat.textContent), null, {timeout: 5000});
100 |   await page.click('#loopRepeat');
101 |   await page.locator('dialog.loop-repeat-editor .loop-keep').click();
102 |   await page.waitForTimeout(2000); // 2.5 passes
103 |   assert(await page.evaluate(id => engine.running && playingPositions.get(id) < 12, djembe), 'kept looping past the preset');
104 |   await page.evaluate(() => stopPlayback());
105 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).loops[0].times, djembe), 2, 'the preset itself is unchanged');
106 | 
107 |   // Clear a count: back to looping until stopped; the mark disappears.
108 |   await shared.dblclick();
109 |   await page.locator('#menu button', {hasText: /结束于此的循环次数/}).click();
110 |   assert.equal(await box.locator('input').inputValue(), '2');
111 |   await box.locator('.loop-keep').click();
112 |   state = await page.evaluate(id => ({loops: doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).loops,
113 |     marks: [...document.querySelectorAll(`.staff[data-track-id="${id}"] .loop-times`)].map(node => node.textContent)}), djembe);
114 |   assert.deepEqual(state.loops, [{from: 0, to: 1}, {from: 1, to: 2, times: 3}]);
115 |   assert.deepEqual(state.marks, ['×3']);
116 |   // Inserting a measure before the loops keeps their counts.
117 |   await page.evaluate(id => { const track = doc.tracks.find(item => item.id === id); change(() => insertTrackBarAt(doc, track, 0), true); }, djembe);
118 |   assert.deepEqual(await page.evaluate(id => doc.tracks.find(track => track.id === id).loops, djembe), [{from: 1, to: 2}, {from: 2, to: 3, times: 3}]);
119 |   console.log('  PASS loop presets: barline and track menus, ×N marks, 2 + 3 passes then automatic continue, chip shows/overrides, save, clear, insert.');
120 | }
121 | 
122 | async function avoidChecks(page, touch) {
123 |   await openDemo(page);
124 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => MENU_HOLD_MS), 500);
125 |   const cdp = touch ? await page.context().newCDPSession(page) : null;
126 |   const doubleTap = async point => {
127 |     if (touch) {
128 |       for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
129 |         await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [{x: point.x, y: point.y}]});
130 |         await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchEnd', touchPoints: []});
131 |         await page.waitForTimeout(60);
132 |       }
133 |     } else await page.mouse.dblclick(point.x, point.y);
134 |     await page.waitForTimeout(250);
135 |   };
136 |   const press = async (point, ms) => {
137 |     if (touch) {
138 |       await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchStart', touchPoints: [{x: point.x, y: point.y}]});
139 |       await page.waitForTimeout(ms);
140 |       await cdp.send('Input.dispatchTouchEvent', {type: 'touchEnd', touchPoints: []});
141 |     } else { await page.mouse.move(point.x, point.y); await page.mouse.down(); await page.waitForTimeout(ms); await page.mouse.up(); }
142 |     await page.waitForTimeout(450);
143 |   };
144 |   const row = page.locator('.staff').nth(2);
145 |   const reset = () => page.evaluate(() => { closePopups(); document.querySelector('dialog[open]')?.close(); });
146 |   const checks = [
147 |     ['barline menu', row.locator('.barline').nth(1), '#menu'],
148 |     ['group meter menu', row.locator('.group-meter').nth(2), '#menu'],
149 |     ['beat menu', row.locator('.slot').nth(4), '#beatMenu'],
150 |     ['sticking menu', row.locator('.slot').nth(5).locator('.hand-dot'), '#menu'],
151 |     ['tempo editor', row.locator('.group-tempo').nth(2), 'dialog.tempo-editor[open]'],
152 |   ];
153 |   for (const [label, locator, menu] of checks) {
154 |     await reset();
155 |     await locator.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
156 |     const point = await centerOf(locator);
157 |     await doubleTap(point);
158 |     assert(await page.locator(menu).isVisible(), label + ' opens on a double-tap');
159 |     assertClear(await rectOf(page, menu), point, label + ' (double-tap)');
160 |   }
161 |   await reset();
162 |   // 0.3 s opens nothing; 0.7 s opens the barline menu.
163 |   const barline = await centerOf(row.locator('.barline').nth(1));
164 |   await press(barline, 300);
165 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => el.menu.hidden), true, 'a 0.3 s press opens nothing');
166 |   await press(barline, 700);
167 |   assert.equal(await page.evaluate(() => el.menu.hidden), false, 'a 0.7 s hold opens the menu');
168 |   assertClear(await rectOf(page, '#menu'), barline, 'barline menu (hold)');
169 |   console.log(`  PASS ${touch ? 'touch' : 'mouse'} (${page.viewportSize().width}px): double-tap menus (barline, group meter, beat, sticking, tempo) clear of the tap; hold opens at 0.5 s, not at 0.3 s.`);
170 | }
171 | 
172 | (async () => {
173 |   const pages = process.argv.slice(2);
174 |   if (!pages.length) throw Error('Usage: node editor163.e2e.cjs PAGE.html [PAGE.html ...]');
175 |   const browser = await chromium.launch({args: ['--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required']});
176 |   try {
177 |     for (const file of pages) {
178 |       console.log('Page ' + path.resolve(file));
179 |       const url = 'file://' + path.resolve(file);
180 |       for (const [label, options] of [['desktop', {viewport: {width: 1280, height: 800}}],
181 |         ['phone', {viewport: {width: 390, height: 780}, hasTouch: true, isMobile: true, deviceScaleFactor: 1}]]) {
182 |         const context = await browser.newContext({...options, locale: 'zh-CN'});
183 |         const page = await context.newPage();
184 |         const errors = [];
185 |         page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
186 |         await page.goto(url);
187 |         await page.waitForSelector('#rhythmGrid');
188 |         if (label === 'desktop') { await presetChecks(page); await avoidChecks(page, false); }
189 |         else { await avoidChecks(page, true); await avoidChecks(page, false); }
190 |         assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'no page errors on ' + label);
191 |         await context.close();
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   } finally { await browser.close(); }
195 |   console.log('PASS 1.63 editor checks on ' + pages.length + ' page(s).');
196 | })().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });

附录 D：新增测试 editor164.e2e.cjs（1.64）

文件：RhythmDesk-Win11/source/tests/editor164.e2e.cjs

  1 | // 1.64 editor checks in a real Chromium page (Playwright): a stem picked in multi-selection, and the
  2 | // paste preview, are framed in translucent blue, so the stem and the note under them stay visible.
  3 | // The check counts dark pixels (stem, beam, note head) inside a stem before and after picking it.
  4 | // Run once per built page:
  5 | //   node editor164.e2e.cjs ../../RhythmDesk.html ANDROID_ASSETS/index.html
  6 | const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
  7 | const path = require('node:path');
  8 | const zlib = require('node:zlib');
  9 | const {chromium} = require('playwright');
 10 | 
 11 | // Minimal PNG reader for Playwright screenshots (8-bit RGB or RGBA, no interlace).
 12 | function readPng(buffer) {
 13 |   let pos = 8, width, height, channels;
 14 |   const data = [];
 15 |   while (pos < buffer.length) {
 16 |     const length = buffer.readUInt32BE(pos), type = buffer.toString('ascii', pos + 4, pos + 8);
 17 |     const chunk = buffer.subarray(pos + 8, pos + 8 + length);
 18 |     if (type === 'IHDR') {
 19 |       width = chunk.readUInt32BE(0); height = chunk.readUInt32BE(4);
 20 |       assert.equal(chunk[8], 8, 'bit depth'); assert.equal(chunk[12], 0, 'no interlace');
 21 |       channels = {2: 3, 6: 4}[chunk[9]];
 22 |       assert(channels, 'colour type ' + chunk[9]);
 23 |     } else if (type === 'IDAT') data.push(chunk);
 24 |     pos += 12 + length;
 25 |   }
 26 |   const raw = zlib.inflateSync(Buffer.concat(data)), stride = width * channels;
 27 |   const pixels = Buffer.alloc(height * stride);
 28 |   for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
 29 |     const filter = raw[y * (stride + 1)], line = raw.subarray(y * (stride + 1) + 1, (y + 1) * (stride + 1));
 30 |     for (let x = 0; x < stride; x++) {
 31 |       const a = x >= channels ? pixels[y * stride + x - channels] : 0;
 32 |       const b = y ? pixels[(y - 1) * stride + x] : 0;
 33 |       const c = x >= channels && y ? pixels[(y - 1) * stride + x - channels] : 0;
 34 |       const p = a + b - c, pa = Math.abs(p - a), pb = Math.abs(p - b), pc = Math.abs(p - c);
 35 |       const predictor = [0, a, b, (a + b) >> 1, pa <= pb && pa <= pc ? a : pb <= pc ? b : c][filter];
 36 |       pixels[y * stride + x] = (line[x] + predictor) & 255;
 37 |     }
 38 |   }
 39 |   return {width, height, channels, pixels};
 40 | }
 41 | // Dark pixels (luminance < 90) inside a box, leaving out a margin for the frame's own border.
 42 | async function darkPixels(page, box, margin = 4) {
 43 |   const png = readPng(await page.screenshot({clip: box}));
 44 |   const scale = png.width / box.width;
 45 |   let dark = 0;
 46 |   for (let y = Math.ceil(margin * scale); y < png.height - margin * scale; y++) {
 47 |     for (let x = Math.ceil(margin * scale); x < png.width - margin * scale; x++) {
 48 |       const i = (y * png.width + x) * png.channels;
 49 |       if (0.299 * png.pixels[i] + 0.587 * png.pixels[i + 1] + 0.114 * png.pixels[i + 2] < 90) dark++;
 50 |     }
 51 |   }
 52 |   return dark;
 53 | }
 54 | 
 55 | async function pickChecks(page) {
 56 |   await page.evaluate(() => { openNewScore(demoProject(), ''); });
 57 |   await page.waitForSelector('.staff');
 58 |   await page.evaluate(() => applyScoreZoom(1));
 59 |   const row = page.locator('.staff').nth(2); // Djembe 1: notes on cells 0 and 2 of the first group
 60 |   await page.evaluate(() => startMultiGroups(doc.tracks[2]));
 61 |   const results = [];
 62 |   for (const index of [0, 2]) {
 63 |     const slot = row.locator('.slot').nth(index);
 64 |     await slot.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
 65 |     const box = await slot.boundingBox();
 66 |     const before = await darkPixels(page, box);
 67 |     await slot.click();
 68 |     await page.waitForTimeout(150);
 69 |     assert(await slot.evaluate(node => node.classList.contains('slot-picked')), 'the stem is picked');
 70 |     const after = await darkPixels(page, box);
 71 |     const style = await slot.evaluate(node => getComputedStyle(node).backgroundColor);
 72 |     results.push({index, before, after, style});
 73 |     assert(before > 40, 'the stem has a drawn stem and note to see: ' + before);
 74 |     assert(after >= before * 0.8, `stem ${index}: the stem and note stay visible through the pick frame (${before} → ${after} dark pixels)`);
 75 |     assert.match(style, /rgba\(40, 118, 232, 0\.1\d+\)/, 'translucent fill: ' + style);
 76 |   }
 77 |   // The paste preview is translucent as well.
 78 |   await page.evaluate(() => {
 79 |     const track = doc.tracks[2];
 80 |     previewMultiPaste(track, 1, 0, copyTrackSpan(doc, track, 0, 3));
 81 |   });
 82 |   const preview = row.locator('.slot.paste-preview').first();
 83 |   assert.match(await preview.evaluate(node => getComputedStyle(node).backgroundColor), /rgba\(40, 118, 232, 0\.1\d+\)/);
 84 |   console.log('  PASS translucent pick frame: ' + results.map(r => `stem ${r.index} ${r.before}→${r.after} dark px`).join(', ') + '; paste preview translucent.');
 85 | }
 86 | 
 87 | (async () => {
 88 |   const pages = process.argv.slice(2);
 89 |   if (!pages.length) throw Error('Usage: node editor164.e2e.cjs PAGE.html [PAGE.html ...]');
 90 |   const browser = await chromium.launch();
 91 |   try {
 92 |     for (const file of pages) {
 93 |       console.log('Page ' + path.resolve(file));
 94 |       for (const options of [{viewport: {width: 900, height: 600}, deviceScaleFactor: 1},
 95 |         {viewport: {width: 390, height: 780}, hasTouch: true, isMobile: true, deviceScaleFactor: 2}]) {
 96 |         const context = await browser.newContext({...options, locale: 'zh-CN'});
 97 |         const page = await context.newPage();
 98 |         const errors = [];
 99 |         page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
100 |         await page.goto('file://' + path.resolve(file));
101 |         await page.waitForSelector('#rhythmGrid');
102 |         await pickChecks(page);
103 |         assert.deepEqual(errors, [], 'no page errors');
104 |         await context.close();
105 |       }
106 |     }
107 |   } finally { await browser.close(); }
108 |   console.log('PASS 1.64 editor checks on ' + pages.length + ' page(s).');
109 | })().catch(error => { console.error(error); process.exitCode = 1; });
